### Setting out for the mosque

#### 1. `hisn-19`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/19.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/19.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">(اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْ فِي قَلْبِي نُوراً، وَفِي لِسَانِي نُوراً، وَفِي سَمْعِي نُوراً، وَفِي بَصَرِي نُوراً، وَمِنْ فَوْقِي نُوراً، وَمِنْ تَحْتِي نُوراً، وَعَنْ يَمِينِي نُوراً، وَعَنْ شِمَالِي نُوراً، وَمِنْ أَمَامِي نُوراً، وَمِنْ خَلْفِي نُوراً، وَاجْعَلْ فِي نَفْسِي نُوراً، وَأَعْظِمْ لِي نُوراً، وَعَظِّم لِي نُوراً، وَاجْعَلْ لِي نُوراً، وَاجْعَلْنِي نُوراً، اللَّهُمَّ أَعْطِنِي نُوراً، وَاجْعَلْ فِي عَصَبِي نُوراً، وَفِي لَحْمِي نُوراً، وَفِي دَمِي نُوراً، وَفِي شَعْرِي نُوراً، وَفِي بَشَرِي نُوراً)([اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْ لِي نُوراً فِي قَبْرِي... وَنُوراً فِي عِظَامِي])[(وَزِدْنِي نُوراً، وَزِدْنِي نُوراً، وَزِدْنِي نُوراً)][(وَهَبْ لِي نُوراً عَلَى نُورٍ)]</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allaahummaj 'al fee qalbee nooran, wa fee lisaanee nooran, wa fee sam'ee nooran, wa fee basaree nooran, wa min fawqee nooran, wa min tahtee nooran, wa 'an yameenee nooran, wa 'an shimaalee nooran, wa min 'amaamee nooran, wa min khalfee nooran. Wa j'al fee nafsee nooran, wa 'a'dhim lee nooran, wa 'a'dhim lee nooran. Waj'al lee nooran, waj'alnee nooran. Allaahumma a'tineje nooran, waj'al fee 'asabee nooran, wa fee lahmee nooran, wa fee damee nooran, wa fee sha'ree nooran, wa fee basharee nooran.[Allaahummaj'al lee nooran fee qabree, wa nooran fee 'idhaamee.][Wa zidnee nooran, wa zidnee nooran, wa zidnee nooran.][Wa hab lee nooran 'alaa noor.]  

**Translation (en)**
> (‘O Allaah, place within my heart light; and upon my tongue light; and within my ears light; and within my eyes light; and place above me light; and beneath me light; and on my right light; and on my left light; and in front of me light; and behind me light; and place light within my soul; and augment light for me; and greaten light for me; and grant me light and make me light. O Allaah, bestow upon me light; and place light in my tendons; and light in my flesh; and light in my blood; and in light in my hair and light in my skin.’ [O Allaah, place light for me in my grave and light in my bones]. [And increase me with light and increase me with light and increase me with light]. [And grant me light upon light.]’)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Setting out for the mosque  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الذهاب إلى المسجد</span>  

---

### Entering the mosque

#### 1. `hisn-20`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/20.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/20.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">(يَبْدَأُ بِرِجْلِهِ الْيُمْنَى) ، وَيَقُولُ: أَعُوذُ بِاللَّهِ العَظِيمِ، وَبِوَجْهِهِ الْكَرِيمِ، وَسُلْطَانِهِ الْقَدِيمِ، مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ الرَّجِيمِ  [بِسْمِ اللَّهِ، وَالصَّلَاةُ] [وَالسَّلَامُ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ]  اللَّهُمَّ افْتَحْ لِي أَبْوَابَ رَحْمَتِكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (a'oothu billahil-'atheem wabiwajhihil-kareem wasultanihil-qadeem minash-shaytanir-rajeem, [bismil-lah, wassalatu] [wassalamu 'ala rasoolil-lah] , allahumma iftah lee abwaba rahmatik.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (I take refuge with Allah, The Supreme and with His Noble Face, and His eternal authority from the accursed devil. In the name of Allah, and prayers and peace be upon the Messenger of Allah.  O Allah, open the gates of Your mercy for me.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Entering the mosque  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء دخول المسجد</span>  

---

### Leaving the mosque

#### 1. `hisn-21`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/21.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/21.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يَبْدَأُ بِرِجْلِهِ الْيُسْرَى  وَيَقُولُ: بِسْمِ اللَّهِ وَالصّلَاةُ وَالسَّلَامُ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ، اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ مِنْ فَضْلِك، اللَّهُمَّ اعْصِمْنِي مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ الرَّجِيمِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismil-lah wassalatu wassalamu 'ala rasoolil-lah, allahumma innee as-aluka min fadlik, allahumma i'simnee minash-shaytanir-rajeem.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (In the name of Allah, and prayers and peace be upon the Messenger of Allah.  O Allah, I ask You from Your favour.  O Allah, guard me from the accursed devil.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Leaving the mosque  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الخروج من المسجد</span>  

---

### Before sleeping

#### 1. `hisn-99`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 3×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/99.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/99.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يَجْمَعُ كَفَّيْهِ ثُمَّ يَنْفُثُ فِيهِمَا فَيَقْرَأُ فِيهِمَا:بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم ﴿قُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ * اللَّهُ الصَّمَدُ* لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ* وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُواً أَحَدٌ﴾.  بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم ﴿قُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ* مِن شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ* وَمِن شَرِّ غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ* وَمِن شَرِّ النَّفَّاثَاتِ فِي الْعُقَدِ* وَمِن شَرِّ حَاسِدٍ إِذَا حَسَدَ﴾.  بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم ﴿قُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ* مَلِكِ النَّاسِ* إِلَهِ النَّاسِ* مِن شَرِّ الْوَسْوَاسِ الْخَنَّاسِ* الَّذِي يُوَسْوِسُ فِي صُدُورِ النَّاسِ* مِنَ الْجِنَّةِ وَ النَّاسِ﴾ ثُمَّ يَمْسَحُ بِهِمَا مَا اسْتَطَاعَ مِنْ جَسَدِهِ يَبْدَأُ بِهِمَا عَلَى رَأْسِهِ وَوَجْهِهِ وَمَا أَقبَلَ مِنْ جَسَدِهِ (يفعلُ ذلك ثلاثَ مرَّاتٍ).</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (When retiring to his bed every night, the Prophet  would hold his palms together, spit (A form of spitting comprising mainly of air with little spittle) in them, recite the last three chapters (Al-Ikhlas, Al-Falaq, An-Nas) of the Quran and then wipe over his entire body as much as possible with his hands, beginning with his head and face and then all parts of the body, he would do this three times.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-100`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/100.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/100.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">﴿اللَّهُ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ هُوَ الْحَيُّ الْقَيُّومُ لاَ تَأْخُذُهُ سِنَةٌ وَلاَ نَوْمٌ لَّهُ مَا فِي السَّمَوَاتِ وَمَا فِي الأَرْضِ مَن ذَا الَّذِي يَشْفَعُ عِنْـــــــــــدَهُ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِهِ يَعْلَمُ مَا بَيْنَ أَيْدِيهِمْ وَمَا خَلْفَهُمْ وَلاَ يُحِيطُونَ بِشَيْءٍ مِّنْ عِلْمِهِ إِلاَّ بِمَا شَاء وَسِعَ كُرْسِيُّهُ السَّمَوَاتِ وَالأَرْضَ وَلاَ يَؤُودُهُ حِفْظُهُمَا وَهُوَ الْعَلِيُّ الْعَظِيمُ﴾.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Recite Ayat-Al-Kursiy (Al-Baqarah :255)  

**Translation (en)**
> Allah - there is no deity except Him, the Ever-Living, the Sustainer of [all] existence. Neither drowsiness overtakes Him nor sleep. To Him belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth. Who is it that can intercede with Him except by His permission? He knows what is [presently] before them and what will be after them, and they encompass not a thing of His knowledge except for what He wills. His Kursi extends over the heavens and the earth, and their preservation tires Him not. And He is the Most High, the Most Great.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-101`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/101.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/101.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">﴿آمَنَ الرَّسُولُ بِمَا أُنزِلَ إِلَيْهِ مِن رَّبِّهِ وَالْمُؤْمِنُونَ كُلٌّ آمَنَ بِاللَّهِ وَمَلآئِكَتِهِ وَكُتُبِهِ وَرُسُلِهِ لاَ نُفَرِّقُ بَيْنَ أَحَدٍ مِّن رُّسُلِهِ وَقَالُواْ سَمِعْنَا وَأَطَعْنَا غُفْرَانَكَ رَبَّنَا وَإِلَيْكَ الْمَصِيرُ* لاَ يُكَلِّفُ اللَّهُ نَفْساً إِلاَّ وُسْعَهَا لَهَا مَا كَسَبَتْ وَعَلَيْهَا مَا اكْتَسَبَتْ رَبَّنَا لاَ تُؤَاخِذْنَا إِن نَّسِينَا أَوْ أَخْطَأْنَا رَبَّنَا وَلاَ تَحْمِلْ عَلَيْنَا إِصْراً كَمَا حَمَلْتَهُ عَلَى الَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِنَا رَبَّنَا وَلاَ تُحَمِّلْنَا مَا لاَ طَاقَةَ لَنَا بِهِ وَاعْفُ عَنَّا وَاغْفِرْ لَنَا وَارْحَمْنَآ أَنتَ مَوْلاَنَا فَانصُرْنَا عَلَى الْقَوْمِ الْكَافِرِينَ﴾.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Recite last two verses of Soorah (Al-Baqarah :285-286)  

**Translation (en)**
> ( 285 )   The Messenger has believed in what was revealed to him from his Lord, and [so have] the believers. All of them have believed in Allah and His angels and His books and His messengers, [saying], We make no distinction between any of His messengers. And they say, We hear and we obey. [We seek] Your forgiveness, our Lord, and to You is the [final] destination.( 286 )   Allah does not charge a soul except [with that within] its capacity. It will have [the consequence of] what [good] it has gained, and it will bear [the consequence of] what [evil] it has earned. Our Lord, do not impose blame upon us if we have forgotten or erred. Our Lord, and lay not upon us a burden like that which You laid upon those before us. Our Lord, and burden us not with that which we have no ability to bear. And pardon us; and forgive us; and have mercy upon us. You are our protector, so give us victory over the disbelieving people.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 4. `hisn-102`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/102.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/102.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِاسْمِكَ رَبِّي وَضَعْتُ جَنْبِي، وَبِكَ أَرْفَعُهُ، فَإِن أَمْسَكْتَ نَفْسِي فارْحَمْهَا، وَإِنْ أَرْسَلْتَهَا فَاحْفَظْهَا، بِمَا تَحْفَظُ بِهِ عِبَادَكَ الصَّالِحِينَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismika rabbee wada'tu janbee wabika arfa'uh, fa-in amsakta nafsee farhamha, wa-in arsaltaha fahfathha bima tahfathu bihi 'ibadakas-saliheen.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (In Your name my Lord, I lie down and in Your name I rise, so if You should take my soul then have mercy upon it, and if You should return my soul then protect it in the manner You do so with Your righteous servants.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 5. `hisn-103`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/103.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/103.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّكَ خَلَقْتَ نَفْسِي وَأَنْتَ تَوَفَّاهَا، لَكَ مَمَاتُهَا وَمَحْياهَا، إِنْ أَحْيَيْتَهَا فَاحْفَظْهَا، وَإِنْ أَمَتَّهَا فَاغْفِرْ لَهَا. اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ العَافِيَةَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma innaka khalaqta nafsee wa-anta tawaffaha, laka mamatuha wamahyaha in ahyaytaha fahfathha, wa-in amattaha faghfir laha. Allahumma innee as-alukal-'afiyah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, verily You have created my soul and You shall take it’s life, to You belongs it’s life and death. If You should keep my soul alive then protect it, and if You should take it’s life then forgive it. O Allah, I ask You to grant me good health.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 6. `hisn-104`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/104.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/104.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ قِنِي عَذَابَكَ يَوْمَ تَبْعَثُ عِبَادَكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma qinee 'athabaka yawma tab'athu 'ibadak.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, protect me from Your punishment on the day Your servants are resurrected.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>

#### 7. `hisn-105`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/105.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/105.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِاسْمِكَ اللَّهُمَّ أَمُوتُ وَأَحْيَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismikal-lahumma amootu wa-ahya.)

#### 8. `hisn-106`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/106.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/106.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ (ثلاثاً وثلاثين) وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ (ثلاثاً وثلاثين) وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ (أربعاً وثلاثينَ).</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Subhanal-lah. (thirty-three times) Alhamdu lillah. (thirty-three times) Allahu akbar. (thirty-four times))  

**Translation (en)**
> (How Perfect Allah is. (thirty-three times) All praise is for Allah.(thirty-three times) Allah is the greatest.(thirty-four times  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 9. `hisn-107`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/107.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/107.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّ السَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعِ وَرَبَّ الأَرْضِ، وَرَبَّ الْعَرْشِ الْعَظِيمِ، رَبَّنَا وَرَبَّ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ، فَالِقَ الْحَبِّ وَالنَّوَى، وَمُنْزِلَ التَّوْرَاةِ وَالْإِنْجِيلِ، وَالْفُرْقَانِ، أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ أَنْتَ آخِذٌ بِنَاصِيَتِهِ. اللَّهُمَّ أَنْتَ الأَوَّلُ فَلَيْسَ قَبْلَكَ شَيْءٌ، وَأَنْتَ الآخِرُ فَلَيسَ بَعْدَكَ شَيْءٌ، وَأَنْتَ الظَّاهِرُ فَلَيْسَ فَوْقَكَ شَيْءٌ، وَأَنْتَ الْبَاطِنُ فَلَيْسَ دُونَكَ شَيْءٌ، اقْضِ عَنَّا الدَّيْنَ وَأَغْنِنَا مِنَ الْفَقْرِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma rabbas-samawatis-sab', warabbal-'arshil-'atheem, rabbana warabba kulli shay/, faliqal-habbi wannawa, wamunazzilat-tawra, wal-injeel, walfurqan, a'oothu bika min sharri kulli shayin anta akhithun binasiyatih. Allahumma antal-awwal, falaysa qablaka shay/, wa-antal-akhir, falaysa ba'daka shay/, wa-antath-thahir falaysa fawqaka shay/, waantal-batin, falaysa doonaka shay/, iqdi 'annad-dayna wa-aghnina minal-faqr.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, Lord of the seven heavens and the exalted throne, our Lord and Lord of all things, Splitter of the seed and the date stone, Revealer of the Tawrah, the Injeel and the Furqan, I take refuge in You from the evil of all things You shall seize by the forelock (i.e. You have total mastery over). O Allah, You are The First so there is nothing before You and You are The Last so there is nothing after You.You are Aththahir so there is nothing above You and You are Al-Batin so there is nothing closer than You.Settle our debt for us and spare us from poverty.)(Tawrah: The book revealed to Moosa u.)(Injeel: The book revealed to Easa u.)(Furqan: One of the many names of the Quran, means: The Criterion which distinguishes between truth and falsehood.)(Aththahir: Indicates the greatness of His attributes and the insignificance of every single creation in respect to His greatness and Highness, for He is above all of His creation as regards His essence and attributes.)(Al-Batin: Indicates His awareness and knowledge of all secrets, of that which is in the hearts and the most intimate of things just as it indicates His closeness and nearness to all in a manner which befits His majesty.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 10. `hisn-108`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/108.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/108.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي أَطْعَمَنَا وَسَقَانَا، وَكَفَانَا، وَآوَانَا، فَكَمْ مِمَّنْ لاَ كَافِيَ لَهُ وَلاَ مُؤْوِيَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Alhamdu lillahil-lathee at'amana wasaqana, wakafana, wa-awana, fakam mimman la kafiya lahu wala mu'wee.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (All praise is for Allah, Who fed us and gave us drink, and Who is sufficient for us and has sheltered us, for how many have none to suffice them or shelter them.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 11. `hisn-109`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/109.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/109.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ عَالِمَ الغَيْبِ وَالشَّهَادَةِ فَاطِرَ السَّمَوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ، رَبَّ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ وَمَلِيكَهُ، أَشْهَدُ أَنْ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ، أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّ نَفْسِي، وَمِنْ شَرِّ الشَّيْطانِ وَشِرْكِهِ، وَأَنْ أَقْتَرِفَ عَلَى نَفْسِي سُوءاً، أَوْ أَجُرَّهُ إِلَى مُسْلِمٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma 'alimal-ghaybi washshahadah, fatiras-samawati wal-ard, rabba kulli shayin wamaleekah, ashhadu an la ilaha illa ant, a'oothu bika min sharri nafsee wamin sharrish-shaytani washirkih, wa-an aqtarifa 'ala nafsee soo-an aw ajurrahu ila muslim.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, Knower of the seen and the unseen, Creator of the heavens and the earth, Lord and Sovereign of all things I bear witness that none has the right to be worshipped except You.  I take refuge in You from the evil of my soul and from the evil and shirk of the devil, and from committing wrong against my soul or bringing such upon another Muslim.)(shirk: to associate others with Allah in those things which are specific to Him.  This can occur in (1) belief, e.g. to believe that other than Allah has the power to benefit or harm, (2) speech, e.g. to swear by other than Allah and (3) action, e.g. to bow or prostrate to other than Allah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>  

---

#### 12. `hisn-110`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/110.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/110.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يَقْرَأُ ﴿الم﴾ تَنْزِيلَ السَّجْدَة ِ،  وَتَبَارَكَ الَّذي بِيَدِهِ الْمُلْكُ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (The Prophet  never used to sleep until he had recited Soorat As-Sajdah (chapter 32) and Soorat Al-Mulk (chapter 67).)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>

#### 13. `hisn-111`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/111.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/111.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَسْلَمْتُ نَفْسِي إِلَيْكَ، وَفَوَّضْتُ أَمْرِي إِلَيْكَ، وَوَجَّهْتُ وَجْهِي إِلَيْكَ، وَأَلْجَأْتُ ظَهْرِي إِلَيْكَ، رَغْبَةً وَرَهْبَةً إِلَيْكَ، لاَ مَلْجَأَ وَلاَ مَنْجَا مِنْكَ إِلاَّ إِلَيْكَ، آمَنْتُ بِكِتَابِكَ الَّذِي أَنْزَلْتَ، وَبِنَبِيِّكَ الَّذِي أَرْسَلْتَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma aslamtu nafsee ilayk, wafawwadtu amree ilayk, wawajjahtu wajhee ilayk, wa-alja/tu thahree ilayk, raghbatan warahbatan ilayk, la maljaa wala manja minka illa ilayk, amantu bikitabikal-lathee anzalt, wabinabiyyikal-lathee arsalt.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, I submit my soul unto You, and I entrust my affair unto You, and I turn my face towards You, and I totally rely on You, in hope and fear of You.Verily there is no refuge nor safe haven from You except with You.  I believe in Your Book which You have revealed and in Your Prophet whom You have sent.)(If you then die, you will die upon the fitrah.)(fitrah: the religion of Islam, the way of Ibraheem u.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before sleeping  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — أذكار النوم</span>

### Turning over in the night

#### 1. `hisn-112`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/112.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/112.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ الْوَاحِدُ الْقَهّارُ، رَبُّ السَّمَوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ وَمَا بَيْنَهُمَا الْعَزيزُ الْغَفَّارُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ilaha illal-lahul-wahidul-qahhar, rabbus-samawati wama baynahuma, al'azeezul-ghaffar.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, The One, AL-Qahhar.Lord of the heavens and the Earth and all between them, The Exalted in Might, The Oft-Forgiving.)(AL-Qahhar:The One Who has subdued all of creation and Whom all of creation are subservient to.  All movements occur by His will.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Turning over in the night  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء إذا تقلب ليلا</span>

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَعُوذُ بِكَلِمَاتِ اللَّهِ التَّامَّاتِ مِنْ غَضَبِهِ وَعِقَابِهِ، وَشَرِّ عِبَادِهِ، وَمِنْ هَمَزَاتِ الشَّياطِينِ وَأَنْ يَحْضُرُونِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Afraid or lonely at night  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الفزع في النوم و من بُلِيَ بالوحشة</span>

> <span dir="rtl">[ (يَنْفُثُ عَنْ يَسَارِهِ (ثلاثاً - (يَسْتَعِيذُ بِاللَّهِ مِنَ الشَّيطَانِ وَمِنْ شَرِّ مَا رَأَى (ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ - لاَ يُحَدِّثْ بِهَا أَحَداً - يَــتَحَوَّلُ عَنْ جَنْبِهِ الَّذِي كَانَ عَلَيْهِ ]</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> [Spit on your left three times)(Spit: A form of spitting comprising mainly of air with little spittle - (Seek refuge in Allah from shaytan and the evil of what you saw (Three Times - (Do not relate it to anyone) - (Turn and sleep on the opposite side to which you were sleeping on previously.) - (Get up and pray if you so desire.) ]  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — After a bad dream  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يفعل من رأى الرؤيا أو الحلم</span>

### Congratulating new parents

#### 1. `hisn-145`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/145.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/145.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بَارَكَ اللَّهُ لَكَ فِي الْمَوْهُوبِ لَكَ، وَشَكَرْتَ الْوَاهِبَ، وَبَلَغَ أَشُدَّهُ، وَرُزِقْتَ بِرَّهُ. وَيَرُدُّ عَلَيْهِ الْمُهَــــــنَّأُ فَيَقُولُ: بَارَكَ اللَّهُ لَكَ وَبَارَكَ عَلَيْكَ، وَجَزَاكَ اللَّهُ خَيْراً، وَرَزَقَكَ اللَّهُ مِثْلَهُ، وَأَجْزَلَ ثَوَابَكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Baarakallaahu laka fil-mawhoobi laka, wa shakartal-waahiba, wa balagha 'ashuddahu, wa ruzigta birrahu.  The reply of the person being congratulated is to say: Baarakallahu laka wa baaraka 'alayka, wa jazaakallaahu khayran, wa razaqakallaahu mithlahu, wa 'ajzala thawaabaka.  

**Translation (en)**
> May Allah bless you with His gift to you and may you (the new parent) give thanks, may the child reach the maturity of years, and may you be granted its righteousness. The reply of the person being congratulated is to say: May Allah bless you, and shower His blessings upon you, and may Allah reward you well and bestow upon you its like and reward you abundantly  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Congratulating new parents  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ﺗﻬنئة المولود له وجوابه</span>  

---

### Seeking protection for children

#### 1. `hisn-146`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/146.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/146.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُعَوِّذُ الحَسَنَ وَالحُسَينَ رضي الله عنهما أُعِيذُكُمَا بِكَلِمَاتِ اللَّهِ التَّامَّةِ مِنْ كُلِّ شَيْطَانٍ وَهَامَّةٍ، وَمِنْ كُلِّ عَيْنٍ لاَمَّةٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Ibn 'abbas related that the Messenger of Allah  used to commend Al-Hasan and Al-Husayn to Allah’s protection, saying:(O'eethukuma bikalimatil-lahit-tammah, min kulli shaytanin wahammah, wamin kulli 'aynin lammah.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Ibn AAabbas related that the Messenger of Allah  used to commend Al-Hasan and Al-Husayn to Allah’s protection, saying:(I commend you two to the protection of Allah’s perfect words from every devil, vermin, and every evil eye.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Seeking protection for children  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يعوذ به الأولاد</span>

### When the wind blows

#### 1. `hisn-166`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/166.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/166.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْــــــأَلُكَ خَيْرَهَا، وَأَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّهَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma innee as-aluka khayraha wa-a'oothu bika min sharriha.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, I ask You for it’s goodness and I take refuge with You from it’s evil.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When the wind blows  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الريح</span>

#### 2. `hisn-167`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/167.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/167.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ خَيْرَهَا، وَخَيْرَ مَا فِيهَا، وَخَيْرَ مَا أُرْسِلَتْ بِهِ، وَأَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّهَا، وَشَرِّ مَا فِيهَا، وَشَرِّ مَا أُرْسِلَتْ بِهِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma innee as-aluka khayraha wakhayra ma feeha, wakhayra ma orsilat bih, wa-a'oothu bika min sharriha, washarri ma feeha washarri ma orsilat bih.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, I ask You for it’s goodness, the good within it, and the good it was sent with, and I take refuge with You from it’s evil, the evil within it, and from the evil it was sent with.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When the wind blows  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الريح</span>

### When the rain will not stop

#### 1. `hisn-174`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/174.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/174.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ حَوَالَيْنَا وَلاَ عَلَيْنَا، اللَّهُمَّ عَلَى الآكَامِ وَالظِّرَابِ، وَبُطُونِ الْأَوْدِيَةِ، وَمَنَابِتِ الشَّجَرِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma hawalayna wala 'alayna, allahumma 'alal-akami waththirab, wabutoonil-awdiyah, wamanabitish-shajar.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, let the rain fall around us and not upon us, O Allah, (let it fall) on the pastures, hills, valleys and the roots of trees.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When the rain will not stop  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — من أدعية الاستصحاء</span>  

---

### Sighting the new moon

#### 1. `hisn-175`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/175.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/175.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُمَّ أَهِلَّهُ عَلَيْنَا بِالْأَمْنِ وَالْإِيمَانِ، وَالسَّلاَمَةِ وَالْإِسْلاَمِ، وَالتَّوْفِيقِ لِمَا تُحِبُّ رَبَّنَا وَتَرْضَى، رَبُّنَا وَرَبُّكَ اللَّهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahu akbar, allahumma ahillahu 'alayna bil-amni wal-eeman, wassalamati wal-islam, wattawfeeiqi lima tuhibbu watarda, rabbuna warabbukal-lah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Allah is the greatest. O Allah, let the crescent loom above us in safety, faith, peace, and Islam, and in agreement with all that You love and pleases You. Our Lord and your Lord is Allah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Sighting the new moon  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء رؤية الهلال</span>

### Breaking the fast

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/176.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/176.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">ذَهَبَ الظَّمَأُ وَابْتَلَّتِ العُرُوقُ، وَثَبَتَ الْأَجْرُ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Thahabath-thama-o wabtallatil-'urooq, wathabatal-ajru in shaal-lah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (The thirst has gone and the veins are quenched, and reward is confirmed, if Allah wills.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Breaking the fast  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند إفطار الصائم</span>

#### 2. `hisn-177`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/177.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/177.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ بِرَحْمَتِكَ الَّتِي وَسِعَتْ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ أَنْ تَغْفِرَ لِي.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma inne as-aluka birahmatikal-latee wasi'at kulla shay, an taghfira lee.)  

**Translation (en)**

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند إفطار الصائم</span>

### Before eating

#### 1. `hisn-178`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/178.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/178.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا أَكَلَ أَحَدُكُمْ طَعَاماً فَلْيَقُلْ بِسْمِ اللَّهِ، فَإِنْ نَسِيَ فِي أَوَّلِهِ فَلْيَقُلْ بسمِ اللَّهِ فِي أَوَّلِهِ وَآخِرِهِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (When you are about to eat, you should say:(Bismil-lah.)and if you forget to say it before starting, then you should say (when you remember):Bismil-lahi fee awwalihi wa-akhirih.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (When you are about to eat, you should say:(Bismil-lah.)and if you forget to say it before starting, then you should say (when you remember):In the name of Allah in it’s beginning and end.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before eating  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء قبل الطعام</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-179`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/179.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/179.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">مَنْ أَطْعَمَهُ اللَّهُ الطَّعَامَ فَلْيَقُلْ: اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِيهِ وَأَطْعِمْنَا خَيْراً مِنْهُ، وَمَنْ سَقَاهُ اللَّهُ لَبَناً فَلْيَقُلْ اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِيهِ وَزِدْنَا مِنْهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Whomever Allah feeds, should say:(Allahumma barik lana feehi wa-at'imna khayran minh.) and whomever Allah gives milk to drink should say:(Allahumma barik lana feehi wazidna minh.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Whomever Allah feeds, should say:(O Allah, bless it for us and feed us better than it.) and whomever Allah gives milk to drink should say:(O Allah, bless it for usand give us more of it.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Before eating  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء قبل الطعام</span>  

---

### After eating

#### 1. `hisn-180`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/180.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/180.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي أَطْعَمَنِي هَذَا، وَرَزَقَنِيهِ، مِنْ غَيْرِ حَوْلٍ مِنِّي وَلاَ قُوَّةٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Alhamdu lillahil-lathee at'amanee hatha warazaqaneehi min ghayri hawlin minnee wala quwwah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (All praise is for Allah who fed me this and provided it for me without any might nor power from myself.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — After eating  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند الفراغ من الطعام</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-181`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/181.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/181.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ حَمْداً كَثِيراً طَيِّباً مُبَارَكاً فِيهِ، غَيْرَ [مَكْفِيٍّ وَلاَ ] مُوَدَّعٍ، وَلاَ مُسْتَغْنَىً عَنْهُ رَبَّنَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Alhamdu lillahi hamdan katheeran tayyiban mubarakan feeh, ghayra makfiyyin wala muwadda'in, wala mustaghnan 'anhu rabbuna.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Allah be praised with an abundant beautiful blessed praise, a never-ending praise, a praise which we will never bid farewell to and an indispensable praise, He is our Lord.)(There are other views as regards to the understanding of this supplication, from them: Allah be praised with an abundant beautiful blessed praise. He is The One Who is sufficient, feeds and is not fed. The One Who is longed for, along with that which is with Him and The One Who is needed, He is our Lord.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — After eating  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند الفراغ من الطعام</span>

### A guest's du'a for the host

#### 1. `hisn-182`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/182.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/182.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَهُمْ فِيمَا رَزَقْتَهُم، وَاغْفِرْ لَهُمْ وَارْحَمْهُمْ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma barik lahum feema razaqtahum, waghfir lahum warhamhum.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, bless for them, that which You have provided them, forgive them and have mercy upon them.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — A guest's du'a for the host  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الضيف لصاحب الطعام</span>

### For someone who gives you a drink

#### 1. `hisn-183`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/183.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/183.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَطْعِمْ مَنْ أَطْعَمَنِي، وَاسْقِ مَنْ سَقَانِي.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma at'im man at'amanee wasqi man saqanee.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, feed him who fed me, and provide with drink him who provided me with drink.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For someone who gives you a drink

### For a family who feeds you at iftar

#### 1. `hisn-184`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/184.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/184.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَفْطَرَ عِنْدَكُمُ الصَّائِمُونَ، وَأَكَلَ طَعَامَكُمُ الْأَبْرَارُ، وَصَلَّتْ عَلَيْكُمُ الْمَلاَئِكَةُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Aftara 'indakumus-sa-imoon, wa-akala ta'amakumul-abrar, wasallat 'alaykumul-mala-ikah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (May the fasting break their fast in your home, and may the dutiful and pious eat your food, and may the angles send prayers upon you.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For a family who feeds you at iftar  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء إذا أفطر عند أهل بيت</span>

### Declining food while fasting

#### 1. `hisn-185`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/185.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/185.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا دُعِيَ أَحَدُكُمْ فَلْيُجِبْ، فَإِنْ كَانَ صَائِماً فَلْيُصَلِّ، وَإِنْ كَانَ مُفْطِراً فَلْيَطْعَمْ ، وَمَعْنَى فَلْيُصَلِّ أَيْ فَلْيَدْعُ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (If you are not invited (to a meal) then answer.  If  you happen to be fasting, then supplicate (for those present) and if you are not fasting, then eat.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Declining food while fasting  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الصائم إذا حضر الطعام ولم يفطر</span>

### The first dates of the season

#### 1. `hisn-187`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/187.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/187.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِي ثَمَرِنَا، وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي مَدِينَتِنَا، وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي صَاعِنَا، وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي مُدِّنَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma barik lana fee thamarina, wabarik lana fee madeenatina, wabarik lana fee sa'ina wabarik lana fee muddina.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, bless our fruit for us, bless our town for us, bless our saAA for us and bless our mudd for us.)(A saAA is equivalent to four mudds and a mudd is equivalent to a dry measure of an average man’s two palms.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The first dates of the season  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند رؤية باكورة الثمر</span>  

---

### Sneezing

#### 1. `hisn-188`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/188.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/188.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا عَطَسَ أَحَدُكُم فَلْيَقُلِ الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلْيَقُلْ لَهُ أَخُوهُ أَوْ صَاحِبُهُ: يَرْحَمُكَ اللَّهُ، فَإِذَا قَالَ لَهُ: يَرحَمُكَ اللَّهُ، فَلْيَقُلْ: يَهْدِيكُمُ اللَّهُ وَيُصْلِحُ بَالَكُمْ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (When one of you sneezes he should say:(Alhamdu lillah.) and his brother or companion should say to him: (Yarhamukal-lah.) and he (i.e. the one who sneezed) replies back to him:(Yahdeekumul-lahu wayuslihu balakum.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (When one of you sneezes he should say:(All praise if for Allah.) and his brother or companion should say to him: (May Allah have mercy upon you.) and he (i.e. the one who sneezed) replies back to him:(May Allah guide you and rectify your condition.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Sneezing  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء العطاس</span>  

---

> (Yahdeekum wayuslihu balakum.)

### For the groom

#### 1. `hisn-190`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/190.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/190.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بَارَكَ اللَّهُ لَكَ، وَبَارَكَ عَلَيْكَ، وَجَمَعَ بَيْنَكُمَا فِي خَيْرٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Barakal-lahu lak, wabaraka 'alayk, wajama'a baynakuma fee khayr.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (May Allah bless for you (your spouse) and bless you, and may He unite both of you in goodness.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For the groom  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للمتزوج</span>

### On the wedding night

#### 1. `hisn-191`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/191.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/191.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا تَزَوَّجَ أَحَدُكُمُ امْرَأَةً، أَوْ إِذَا اشْتَرَى خَادِماً فَلْيَقُلْ: اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ خَيْرَهَا، وَخَيْرَ مَا جَبَلْتَهَا عَلَيْهِ، وَأَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّهَا، وَشَرِّ مَا جَبَلْتَهَا عَلَيْهِ، وَإِذَا اشْتَرَى بَعِيراً فَلْيَأْخُذْ بِذِرْوَةِ سَنَامِهِ وَلْيَقُلْ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (when you marry a woman or buy a maidservant, you should say: (Allahumma innee as-aluka khayraha wakhayra ma jabaltaha 'alayh, wa-a'oothu bika min sharriha washarri ma jabaltaha 'alayh.))(and if you buy a camel, then you should take hold of it’s hump and say likewise.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (when you marry a woman or buy a maidservant, you should say:(O Allah, I ask You for the goodness within her and the goodness that you have made her inclined towards, and I take refuge with You from the evil within her and the evil that you have made her inclined towards.(and if you buy a camel, then you should take hold of it’s hump and say likewise.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — On the wedding night  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء المتزوج و شراء الدابة</span>  

---

### Before intimacy

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/192.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/192.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِسْمِ اللَّهِ، اللَّهُمَّ جَنِّبْنَا الشَّيْطَانَ، وَجَنِّبِ الشَّيْطَانَ مَا رَزَقْتَنَا.</span>

> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء قبل إتيان الزوجة</span>

### Sitting in a gathering

#### 1. `hisn-195`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/195.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/195.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنه قَاَلَ: كَانَ يُعَدُّ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي الْمَجْلِسِ الوَاحِدِ مِائَةُ مَرَّةٍ مِنْ قَبْلِ أَنْ يَقُومَ: رَبِّ اغْفِرْ لِي، وَتُبْ عَلَيَّ، إِنَّكَ أَنْتَ التَّوَّابُ الغَفُورُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Ibn 'umar  said: It would be counted that the Messenger of Allah  would say one hundred times at any one sitting before getting up: (Rabbigh-fir lee watub 'alay, innaka antat-tawwabul-ghafoor.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Ibn AAumar  said: It would be counted that the Messenger of Allah  would say one hundred times at any one sitting before getting up: (O my Lord, forgive me and turn towards me (to accept my repentance). Verily You are The Oft-Returning. The Oft-Forgiving.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Sitting in a gathering  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقال في اﻟﻤﺠلس</span>

### For someone who says 'may Allah bless you'

#### 1. `hisn-204`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/204.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/204.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَفِيكَ بَارَكَ اللَّهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Aaishah reported that the Messenger of Allah  was given a sheep and he ordered for it’s distribution.  When the servant would come back (from distributing it), 'aishah would ask: ‘What did they say?’, he replied: They would supplicate: (Barakal-lahu feekum.) 'aishah would then say: (Wafeehim barakal-lah.))(we return their supplication in a similar way and our reward remains with us.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Aaishah reported that the Messenger of Allah  was given a sheep and he ordered for it’s distribution.  When the servant would come back (from distributing it), AAaishah would ask: ‘What did they say?’, he replied: They would supplicate: (May Allah bless you all.) AAaishah would then say: (and may Allah bless them.(we return their supplication in a similar way and our reward remains with us.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For someone who says 'may Allah bless you'  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء لمن قال بارك الله فيك</span>

### Riding

#### 1. `hisn-206`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/206.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/206.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِسْمِ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ للَّهِ ﴿سُبْحَانَ الَّذِي سَخَّرَ لَنَا هَذَا وَمَا كُنَّا لَهُ مُقْرِنِينَ، وَإِنَّا إِلَى رَبِّنَا لَمُنقَلِبُونَ﴾، الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، سُبْحَانَكَ اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي ظَلَمْتُ نَفْسِي فَاغْفِرْ لِي؛ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismil-lah, walhamdu lillah, subhanal-lathee sakhkhara lana hatha wama kunna lahu muqrineen, wainna ila rabbina lamunqaliboon, alhamdu lillah, alhamdu lillah, alhamdu lillah, Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, subhanakal-lahumma innee thalamtu nafsee faghfir lee fainnahu la yaghfiruth-thunooba illa ant.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (In the name of Allah and all praise is for Allah.  How perfect He is, the One Who has placed this (transport) at our service and we ourselves would not have been capable of that, and to our Lord is our final destiny.  All praise is for Allah, All praise is for Allah, All praise is for Allah,  Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest. How perfect You are, O Allah, verily I have wronged my soul, so forgive me, for surely none can forgive sins except You.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Riding  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الركوب</span>  

---

### Travelling

#### 1. `hisn-207`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/207.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/207.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، ﴿سُبْحَانَ الَّذِي سَخَّرَ لَنَا هَذَا وَمَا كُنَّا لَهُ مُقْرِنِينَ * وَإِنَّا إِلَى رَبِّنَا لَمُنقَلِبُونَ﴾ اللَّهُمَّ إِنّا نَسْأَلُكَ فِي سَفَرِنَا هَذَا البِرَّ وَالتَّقْوَى، وَمِنَ الْعَمَلِ مَا تَرْضَى، اللَّهُمَّ هَوِّنْ عَلَيْنَا سَفَرَنَا هَذَا وَاطْوِ عَنَّا بُعْدَهُ، اللَّهُمَّ أَنْتَ الصَّاحِبُ فِي السَّفَرِ، وَالْخَليفَةُ فِي الْأَهْلِ، اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ وَعْثَاءِ السَّفَرِ، وَكَآبَةِ الْمَنْظَرِ، وَسُوءِ الْمُنْقَلَبِ فِي الْمَالِ وَالْأَهْلِ، وإذا رَجَعَ قَالَهُنَّ وَزَادَ فِيهِنَّ: آيِبُونَ، تائِبُونَ، عَابِدُونَ، لِرَبِّنَا حَامِدُونَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, subhanal-lathee sakhkhara lana hatha wama kunna lahu muqrineen, wa-inna ila rabbina lamunqaliboon, allahumma inna nas-aluka fee safarina hatha albirra wattaqwa, waminal-'amali ma tarda, allahumma hawwin 'alayna safarana hatha, watwi 'anna bu'dah, allahumma antas-sahibu fis-safar, walkhaleefatu fil-ahl, allahumma innee a'oothu bika min wa'tha-is-safar, waka-abatil-manthar, wasoo-il-munqalabi fil-mali wal-ahl.)(upon returning the same supplication is recited with the following addition: (Ayiboona, ta-iboona, 'abidoona, lirabbina hamidoon.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest, How perfect He is, The One Who has placed this (transport) at our service, and we ourselves would not have been capable of that, and to our Lord is our final destiny.  O Allah, we ask You for birr and taqwa in this journey of ours, and we ask You for deeds which please You.  O Allah, facilitate our journey and let us cover it’s distance quickly.  O Allah, You are The Companion on the journey and The Successor over the family, O Allah, I take refuge with You from the difficulties of travel, from having a change of hearts and being in a bad predicament, and I take refuge in You from an ill fated outcome with wealth and family.)(birr and taqwa: two comprehensive terms which individually, refer to all good actions and obedience i.e. performing the commanded actions and avoiding the prohibited actions.  When combined together, birr refers to doing those actions which have been commanded and taqwa refers to avoiding those actions which have been prohibited.)(A successor: one who succeeds another due to the latter’s absence or death.  This is the correct meaning of the word khaleefah; thus, it is incorrect to believe that Adam is the khaleefah (vicegerent, as is commonly translated) of Allah on earth because Allah is never absent, and will never die.  This supplication proves the correct understanding of this term and shows that Allah succeeds us and guards whom we leave behind when we die or are absent.)(upon returning the same supplication is recited with the following addition: (We return, repent, worship and praise our Lord.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Travelling  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء السفر</span>  

---

### Entering a town

#### 1. `hisn-208`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/208.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/208.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّ السَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعِ وَمَا أَظْلَلْنَ، وَرَبَّ الأَرَضِينَ السَّبْعِ وَمَا أَقْلَلْنَ، وَرَبَّ الشَّياطِينِ وَمَا أَضْلَلْنَ، وَرَبَّ الرِّيَاحِ وَمَا ذَرَيْنَ، أَسْأَلُكَ خَيْرَ هَذِهِ الْقَرْيَةِ، وَخَيْرَ أَهْلِهَا، وَخَيْرَ مَا فِيهَا، وَأَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ شَرِّهَا، وَشَرِّ أَهْلِهَا، وَشَرِّ مَا فِيهَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma rabbas-samawatis-sab'i wama athlaln, warabbal-aradeenas-sab'i wama aqlaln, warabbash-shayateeni wama adlaln, warabbar-riyahi wama tharayn, as-aluka khayra hathihil-qaryah, wakhayra ahlilha wakhayra ma feeha, wa-a'oothu bika min sharriha washarri ahliha, washarri ma feeha.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, Lord of the seven heavens and all that they envelop, Lord of the seven earths and all that they carry, Lord of the devils and all whom they misguide, Lord of the winds and all whom they whisk away. I ask You for the goodness of this village, the goodness of its inhabitants and for all the goodness found within it and I take refuge with You from the evil of this village, the evil of its inhabitants and from all the evil found within it.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Entering a town  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء دخول القرية أو البلدة</span>  

---

### Entering the market

#### 1. `hisn-209`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/209.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/209.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ، وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، يُحْيِي وَيُمِيتُ، وَهُوَ حَيٌّ لاِ يَمُوتُ، بِيَدِهِ الْخَيْرُ، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ilaha illal-lah,  wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, yuhyee wayumeetu wahuwa hayyun la yamoot, biyadihil-khayru wahuwa  'ala kulli shayin qadeer.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner, to Him belongs all sovereignty and praise.  He gives life and causes death, and He is living and does not die.  In His hand is all good and He is over all things, omnipotent.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Entering the market  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء دخول السوق</span>

### A traveller at dawn

#### 1. `hisn-215`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/215.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/215.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">سَمَّعَ سَامِعٌ بِحَمْدِ اللَّهِ، وَحُسْنِ بَلاَئِهِ عَلَيْنَا، رَبَّنَا صاحِبْنَا، وَأَفْضِلْ عَلَيْنَا، عَائِذاً بِاللَّهِ مِنَ النَّارِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Sami'a sami'un bihamdil-lahi wahusni bala-ihi 'alayna. Rabbana sahibna wa-afdil 'alayna 'a-ithan billahi minan-nar.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (May a witness, be witness to our praise of Allah for His favours and bounties upon us.  Our Lord, protect us, show favour on us and deliver us from every evil.  I take refuge in Allah from the fire.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — A traveller at dawn  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء المسافر إذا أسحر</span>  

---

### Stopping along the way

#### 1. `hisn-216`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/216.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/216.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَعُوذُ بِكَلِمَاتِ اللَّهِ التَّامَّاتِ مِنْ شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (a'oothu bikalimatil-lahit-tammati min sharri ma khalaq.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (I take refuge in Allah’s perfect words from the evil that He has created.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Stopping along the way  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء إذا نزل مترلا في سفر أو غيره</span>

### Returning from a journey

#### 1. `hisn-217`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/217.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/217.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يُكَبِّرُ عَلَى كُلِّ شَرَفٍ ثَلاَثَ تَكْبِيرَاتٍ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ، وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ، آيِبُونَ، تَائِبُونَ، عَابِدُونَ، لِرَبِّنا حَامِدُونَ، صَدَقَ اللَّهُ وَعْدَهُ، وَنَصَرَ عَبْدَهُ، وَهَزَمَ الْأَحْزابَ وَحْدَهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Ibn 'umar  reported that the Messenger of Allah  on return from a battle or from performing the pilgrimage would say at every high point: (Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar.) and then he would say: (La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shay-in qadeer, ayiboona ta-iboon, 'abidoon, lirabbina hamidoon, sadaqal-lahu wa'dah, wanasara 'abdah, wahazamal-ahzaba wahdah.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Ibn AAumar  reported that the Messenger of Allah  on return from a battle or from performing the pilgrimage would say at every high point: (Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest.) and then he would say: (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner.  To Him belongs all sovereignty and praise, and He is over all things omnipotent.  We return, repent, worship and praise our Lord.  Allah fulfilled His promise, aided His Servant, and single-handedly defeated the allies.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Returning from a journey  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ذكر الرجوع من السفر</span>  

---

### When something pleases or displeases you

#### 1. `hisn-218`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/218.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/218.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">كَانَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا أَتَاهُ الْأَمْرُ يَسُرُّهُ قَالَ: الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي بِنِعْمَتِهِ تَتِمُّ الصَّالِحَاتُ وَإِذَا أَتَاهُ الْأَمْرُ يَكْرَهُهُ قَالَ: الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ عَلَى كُلِّ حَالٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> When something happened that pleased him, the Prophet used to say:((Alhamdu lillaahil-lathee bini'matihi tatimmuus-saalihaat)).And if something happened that displeased him, he used to say:((Alhamdulillaahi 'alaa kulli haal)).  

**Translation (en)**
> When something happened that pleased him, the Prophet used to say:Alhamdu lillaahil-lathee bini'matihi tatimmuus-saalihaat. Praise is to Allah Who by His blessings all good things are perfected. And if something happened that displeased him, he used to say:Alhamdulillaahi 'alaa kulli haal.Praise is to Allah in all circumstances.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When something pleases or displeases you  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول من أتاه أمر يسره أو يكرهه</span>

### The excellence of asking blessings on the Prophet ﷺ

#### 1. `hisn-219`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/219.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/219.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: مَنْ صَلَّى عَلَيَّ صَلاَةً صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ بِهَا عَشْراً.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (The Prophet (PBUH) said: ‘Whoever sends a prayer upon me, Allah sends ten upon him.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of asking blessings on the Prophet ﷺ  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل الصلاة على النبي صلى الله عليه و سلم</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-220`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/220.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/220.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: لاَ تَجْعَلُوا قَبْرِي عِيداً وَصَلُّوا عَلَيَّ؛ فَإِنَّ صَلاَتَكُم تَبْلُغُنِي حَيْثُ كُنْتُمْ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He (PBUH) also said: ‘Do not take my grave as a place of habitual ceremony.  Send prayers upon me, for verily your prayers reach me wherever you are.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of asking blessings on the Prophet ﷺ  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل الصلاة على النبي صلى الله عليه و سلم</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-221`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/221.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/221.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: الْبَخِيلُ مَنْ ذُكِرْتُ عِنْدَهُ فَلَمْ يُصَلِّ عَلَيَّ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He (PBUH) also said: ‘A miser is one whom when I am mentioned to him,  fails to send prayers upon me.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of asking blessings on the Prophet ﷺ  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل الصلاة على النبي صلى الله عليه و سلم</span>  

---

#### 4. `hisn-222`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/222.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/222.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم:إِنَّ لِلَّهِ مَلاَئِكَةً سَيَّاحِينَ فِي الْأَرْضِ يُبَلِّغُونِي مِنْ أُمَّتِي السَّلاَمَ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> The Prophet [PBUH] said:'Indeed Allah has angels who roam the earth and they convey to me the greetings (or prayers of peace) of my 'Ummah (nation).

**Reference (ar)**

**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: مَا مِنْ أَحَدٍ يُسَلِّمُ عَلَيَّ إِلاَّ رَدَّ اللَّهُ عَلَيَّ رُوحِيَ حَتَّى أَرُدَّ عَلَيْهِ السَّلاَمَ.</span>

### Spreading the salam

#### 1. `hisn-224`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/224.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/224.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: لاَ تَدْخُلُوا الْجَنَّةَ حَتَّى تُؤْمِنُوا، وَلاَ تُؤْمِنُوا حَتَّى تَحَابُّوا، أَوَلاَ أَدُلُّكُم عَلَى شَيْءٍ إِذَا فَعَلْتُمُوهُ تَحَابَبْتُم، أَفْشُوا السَّلاَمَ بَيْنَكُمْ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (The Messenger of Allah  said: ‘You shall not enter paradise until you believe, and you shall not believe until you love one another. Shall I not inform you of something, if you were to act upon it, you will indeed achieve mutual love for one another?  Spread the greeting amongst yourselves.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Spreading the salam  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — إفشاء السلام</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-225`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/225.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/225.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">ثَلاَثٌ مَنْ جَمَعَهُنَّ فَقَدْ جَمَعَ الْإِيمَانَ: الْإِنْصَافُ مِنْ نَفْسِكَ، وَبَذْلُ السَّلاَمِ لِلْعَالَمِ، وَالْإِنْفَاقُ مِنَ الإِقْتَارِ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Aammar  said: ‘Three characteristics, whoever combines them, has completed his faith: to be just, to spread greetings to all people and to spend (charitably) out of the little you have.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Spreading the salam  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — إفشاء السلام</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-226`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/226.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/226.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ عَنْهُمَا: أنَّ رَجُلاً سَأَلَ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أيُّ الْإِسْلاَمِ خَيْرٌ قَالَ: تُطْعِمُ الطَّعَامَ، وَتَقْرأُ السَّلاَمَ عَلَى مَنْ عَرَفْتَ وَمَنْ لَمْ تَعْرِفْ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Aabdullah Ibn Aamr  reported that a man asked the Prophet : ‘Which Islam is the best?’.  He  replied: Feed (the poor), and greet those whom you know as well as those whom you do not.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Spreading the salam  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — إفشاء السلام</span>

### Hearing a rooster or a donkey

#### 1. `hisn-228`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/228.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/228.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا سَمِعْتُمْ صِيَاحَ الدِّيَكَةِ فَاسْأَلُوا اللَّهَ مِنْ فَضْلِهِ؛ فَإِنَّهَا رَأَتْ مَلَكاً وَإِذَا سَمِعْتُمْ نَهِيقَ الْحِمَارِ فَتَعَوَّذُوا بِاللَّهِ مِنَ الشَّيطَانِ؛ فَإِنَّهُ رَأَى شَيْطَاناً.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (If you hear the crow of a rooster, ask Allah for his bounty for it has seen an angel and if you hear the braying of an ass, seek refuge in Allah for it has seen a devil.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Hearing a rooster or a donkey  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدُّعاءُ عِنْدَ سَمَاعِ صِياحِ الدِّيكِ ونَهِيقِ الْحِمَارِ</span>

### Hearing a dog bark at night

#### 1. `hisn-229`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/229.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/229.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا سَمِعْتُمْ نُبَاحَ الْكِلاَبِ وَنَهِيقَ الْحَمِيرِ بِاللَّيْلِ فَتَعَوَّذُوا بِاللَّهِ مِنْهُنَّ؛ فَإِنَّهُنَّ يَرَيْنَ مَا لاَ تَرَوْنَ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (If you hear the barking of dogs or the braying of asses at night, seek refuge in Allah for they see what you do not.)

### Praising another Muslim

#### 1. `hisn-231`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/231.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/231.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: إِذَا كَانَ أَحَدُكُم مَادِحاً صَاحِبَهُ لاَ مَحَالَةَ فَلْيَقُلْ: أَحْسِبُ فُلاَناً وَاللَّهُ حَسِيبُهُ، وَلاَ أُزَكِّي عَلَى اللَّهِ أَحَداً، أَحْسِبُهُ – إِنْ كَانَ يَعْلَمُ ذَاكَ – كَذَا وَكَذَا.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He  said: ‘If anyone of you is impelled to praise his brother, then he should say: ‘I deem so-and-so to be…and Allah is his reckoner…and I don’t praise anyone, putting it (i.e. my praising) forward, in front of Allah’s commendation, however I assume him so and so’…if he knows that of him.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Praising another Muslim  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول المسلم إذا مدح المسلم</span>  

---

### When you are praised

#### 1. `hisn-232`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/232.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/232.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ لاَ تُؤَاخِذْنِي بِمَا يَقُولُونَ، وَاغْفِرْ لِي مَا لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ، [وَاجْعَلْنِي خَيْراً مِمَّا يَظُّنُّونَ].</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allaahumma laa tu'aakhithnee bimaa yaqooloona, waghfir lee maa laa ya'lamoona [waj'alnee khayram-mimmaa yadhunnoon]  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, do not call me to account for what they say and forgive me for what they have no knowledge of [and make me better than they imagine].  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When you are praised  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول المسلم إذا زكي</span>  

---

### The talbiyah

#### 1. `hisn-233`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/233.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/233.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لَبَّيْكَ اللَّهُمَّ لَبَّيْكَ، لَبَّيْكَ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَكَ لَبَّيْكَ، إِنَّ الْحَمْدَ، وَالنِّعْمَةَ، لَكَ وَالْمُلْكَ، لاَ شَرِيكَ لَكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Labbaykal-lahumma labbayk, labbayka la shareeka laka labbayk, innal-hamda wanni'mata laka walmulk, la shareeka lak.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Here I am O Allah, (in response to Your call), here I am. Here I am, You have no partner, here I am.  Verily all praise, grace and sovereignty belong to You. You have no partner.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The talbiyah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — كيف يلبي المحرم في الحج أو العمرة ؟</span>

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)

> <span dir="rtl">طَافَ النَّبيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْبَيْتِ عَلَى بَعِيرٍ كُلَّمَا أَتَى الرُّكْنَ أَشَارَ إِلَيْهِ بِشَيْءٍ عِنْدَهُ وَكَبَّرَ.</span>

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Passing the Black Stone

> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — التكبير إذا أتى الركن الأسود</span>

### Between the Yemeni Corner and the Black Stone

#### 1. `hisn-235`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/235.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/235.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">﴿رَبَّنَا آتِنَا فِي الدُّنْيَا حَسَنَةً وَفِي الْآخِرَةِ حَسَنَةً وَقِنَا عَذَابَ النَّارِ﴾.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> The Prophet PBUH used to say between the Yemeni corner and the black stone: (Rabbana atina fee alddunya hasanatan wafee al-akhirati hasanatan waqina  'athaba alnnar)[Al-Baqarah: 201]  

**Translation (en)**
> The Prophet PBUH used to say between the Yemeni corner and the black stone: (O our Lord, grant us the best in this life and the best in the next life, and protect us from the punishment of the Fire.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Between the Yemeni Corner and the Black Stone  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء بين الركن اليماني والحجر الأسود</span>  

---

### Standing at Safa and Marwah

#### 1. `hisn-236`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/236.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/236.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لَمَّا دَنَا النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنَ الصَّفَا قَرَأَ: ﴿إِنَّ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةَ مِنْ شَعَآئِرِ اللَّهِ﴾ أَبْدَأُ بِمَا بَدَأَ اللَّهُ بِهِ فَبَدَأَ بِالصَّفَا فَرَقِيَ عَلَيْهِ حَتَّى رَأَى الْبَيْتَ، فَاسْتَقْبَلَ الْقِبْلَةَ، فَوَحَّدَ اللَّهَ وَكبَّرَهُ وَقَالَ: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ، أَنْجَزَ وَعْدَهُ، وَنَصَرَ عَبْدَهُ، وَهَزَمَ الْأَحْزَابَ وَحْدَهُ، ثُمَّ دَعَا بَيْنَ ذلكَ. قَالَ مِثْلَ هَذَا ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ الْحَدِيثُ. وَفِيهِ: فَفَعَلَ عَلَى الْمَرْوَةِ كَمَا فَعَلَ عَلَى الصَّفَا.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Jabir  said when describing the Prophet’s  pilgrimage: ‘…and when he approached mount Safa he recited: ( Innas-safa wa-almarwata min sha'a-iri Allah ….) (Abda-o bima badaal-lahu bih.) so he started with Safa and climed it until he could see the Ka'bah, he then faced it and said: (Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar, Allahu akbar.) and then he would say the following three times making a supplication (one should make a personal supplication) after each time: (La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shayin qadeer, la ilaha illal-lahu wahdah, anjaza wa'dah, wanasara 'abdah, wahazamal -ahzaba wahdah.)(he  would repeat this action at Marwah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Jabir  said when describing the Prophet’s  pilgrimage: ‘…and when he approached mount Safa he recited: (Indeed Safa and Marwah are from the places of worship of Allah…) (I begin with what Allah began with.) so he started with Safa and climed it until he could see the KaAAbah, he then faced it and said: (Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest, Allah is the greatest.) and then he would say the following three times making a supplication (one should make a personal supplication) after each time: (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner.To Him belongs all sovereignty and praise and He is over all things amnipotent. None has the right to be worshipped except Allah alone.  He fulfilled His promise, aided His Servant and single-handedly defeated the allies. (he  would repeat this action at Marwah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Standing at Safa and Marwah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الوقوف على الصفا والمروة</span>

### The Day of 'Arafah

#### 1. `hisn-237`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/237.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/237.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: خَيْرُ الدُّعَاءِ دُعَاءُ يَوْمِ عَرَفَةَ، وَخَيْرُ مَا قُلْتُ أَنَا وَالنَّبيُّونَ مِنْ قَبْلِي: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (The best of supplications is the supplication on the day of 'arafah and the best which I and the Prophets before me have said (is): (La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shayin qadeer.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (The best of supplications is the supplication on the day of AAarafah and the best which I and the Prophets before me have said (is): (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner. To Him belongs all praise and sovereignty and He is over all things omnipotent.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The Day of 'Arafah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء يوم عرفة</span>  

---

### At Muzdalifah

#### 1. `hisn-238`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/238.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/238.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَكِبَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْقَصْوَاءَ حَتَّى أَتَى الْمَشْعَرَ الْحَرَامَ فَاسْتَقْبَلَ الْقِبْلَةَ (فَدَعَاهُ، وَكَبَّرَهُ، وَهَللَّهُ، وَوَحَّدَهُ) فَلَمْ يَزَلْ وَاقِفاً حَتَّى أَسْفَرَ جِدَّاً فَدَفَعَ قَبْلَ أَنْ تَطْلُعَ الشَّمسُ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> The Prophet rode his camel, Al-Qaswa, until he reached the sacred area (Al-Mash aril Haraam). Then he faced the Qiblah and invoked Allah, and repeatedly said the words: Allaahu Akbar (Allah is the Most Great), Allaahu Ahad (Allah is One) and Laa ilaaha illallaah (There is none worthy of worship but Allah). He remained stationary until the sky became yellow with the dawn, and then pressed on before sunrise.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — At Muzdalifah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الذكر عند المشعر الحرام</span>  

---

### Stoning the pillars at Mina

#### 1. `hisn-239`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/239.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/239.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يُكَبِّرُ كُلَّمَا رَمَى بِحَصَاةٍ عِنْدَ الْجِمَارِ الثَّلاَثِ، ثُمَّ يَتَقَدَّمُ، ويَقِفُ يَدْعُو مُسْتَقْبِلَ الْقِبلَةِ، رَافِعاً يَدَيْهِ بَعْدَ الْجَمْرَةِ الْأُولَى وَالثَّانِيَةِ. أَمَّا جَمْرَةُ الْعَقَبَةِ فَيَرْمِيهَا وَيُكَبِّرُ عِنْدَ كُلِّ حَصَاةٍ وَيَنْصَرِفُ وَلاَ يَقِفُ عِنْدَهَا.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> The Prophet PBUH said Allaahu 'Akbar (Allah is the Most Great) with each pebble he threw at the three pillars. Then he went forward, stood facing the Qiblah and raised his hands and called upon Allah. That was after (stoning) the first and second pillar As for the third, he stoned it and called out Allahu Akbar with every pebble he threw but when he was finished he left without standing at it (for supplications).  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Stoning the pillars at Mina  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — التكبير عند رمي الجمار مع كل حصاة</span>  

---

### When something delights you

#### 1. `hisn-242`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/242.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/242.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">كَانَ النَّبيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا أَتَاهُ أَمْرٌ يَسُرُّهُ أَوْ يُسَرُّ بِهِ خَرَّ سَاجِداً شُكْراً لِلَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (The Prophet PBUH would prostrate in gratitude to Allah upon receiving news which pleased him or which caused pleasure. )  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When something delights you  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يفعل من أتاه أمر يسره</span>

### Slaughtering

#### 1. `hisn-246`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/246.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/246.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِسْمِ اللَّهِ وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ [اللَّهُمَّ مِنْكَ وَلَكَ] اللَّهُمَّ تَقَبَّلْ مِنِّي.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismil-lah wallahu akbar, allahumma minka walak, allahumma taqabbal  minnee.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (In the name of Allah, and Allah is the greatest.  O Allah, (it is) from You and belongs to You, O Allah, accept this from me.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Slaughtering  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول عند الذبح أو النحر</span>

### Repentance and seeking forgiveness

#### 1. `hisn-248`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/248.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/248.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: وَاللَّهِ إِنِّي لأَسْتَغفِرُ اللَّهَ وَأَتُوبُ إِلَيْهِ فِي الْيَوْمِ أَكْثَرَ مِنْ سَبْعِينَ مَرَّةٍ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (The Messenger of Allah  said: ‘By Allah, I seek forgiveness and repent to Allah, more than seventy times a day.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Repentance and seeking forgiveness  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الاستغفار و التوبة</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-249`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/249.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/249.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: يَا أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ تُوبُوا إِلَى اللَّهِ فَإِنِّي أَتُوبُ فِي الْيَوْمِ إِلَيْهِ مِائَةَ مَرَّةٍ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He  also said: ‘O People, Repent! Verily I repent to Allah, a hundred times a day.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Repentance and seeking forgiveness  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الاستغفار و التوبة</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-250`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/250.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/250.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: مَنْ قَالَ أَسْتَغْفِرُ اللَّهَ الْعَظيمَ الَّذِي لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ هُوَ الْحَيُّ القَيّوُمُ وَأَتُوبُ إِلَيهِ، غَفَرَ اللَّهُ لَهُ وَإِنْ كَانَ فَرَّ مِنَ الزَّحْفِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (He  also said: ‘Whoever says:(Astaghfirul-lahal-lathee la ilaha illa huwal-hayyul-qayyoomu wa-atoobu ilayh.) Allah would forgive him even if he was one who fled during the advance of an army.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (He  also said: ‘Whoever says:(I seek Allah’s forgiveness, besides whom, none has the right to be worshipped except He, The Ever Living, The Self-Subsisting and Supporter of all, and I turn to Him in repentance.) Allah would forgive him even if he was one who fled during the advance of an army.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Repentance and seeking forgiveness  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الاستغفار و التوبة</span>

#### 4. `hisn-251`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/251.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/251.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: أَقْرَبُ مَا يَكُونُ الرَّبُّ مِنَ الْعَبْدِ فِي جَوْفِ اللَّيْلِ الآخِرِ فَإِنِ اسْتَطَعْتَ أَنْ تَكُونَ مِمَّنْ يَذْكُرُ اللَّهَ فِي تِلْكَ السَّاعَةِ فَكُنْ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He  said: ‘The nearest the Lord comes to His servant is in the middle of the night, so if you are able to be of those who remember Allah at that time, then be so.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Repentance and seeking forgiveness  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الاستغفار و التوبة</span>

#### 6. `hisn-253`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/253.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/253.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: إِنَّهُ لَيُغَانُ عَلَى قَلْبِي وَإِنِّي لأَسْتَغْفِرُ اللَّهَ فِي الْيَوْمِ مِائَةَ مَرَّةٍ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (He  also said: ‘verily my heart becomes preoccupied, and verily I seek Allah’s forgiveness a hundred times a day.)(preoccupied: i.e. in a state of ‘forgetfulness’. The Prophet  always used to increase in his remembrance of his Lord, in attaining a nearness to Allah and having consciousness of Allah to the extent that if this intensity lessened in anyway, he would regard it as a sin and would then race to seek forgiveness from Allah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Repentance and seeking forgiveness  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الاستغفار و التوبة</span>
