### The excellence of remembering Allah

#### 1. `hisn-254`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 100×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/254.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/254.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَنْ قَالَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ فِي يَوْمٍ مِائَةَ مَرَّةٍ حُطَّتْ خَطَايَاهُ وَلَوْ كَانَتْ مِثْلَ زَبَدِ الْبَحْر.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Whoever says:(Subhanal-lahi wabihamdih.) a hundred times during the day, his sins are wiped away, even if they are like the foam of the sea.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Whoever says:(How perfect Allah is and I praise Him.) a hundred times during the day, his sins are wiped away, even if they are like the foam of the sea.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-255`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 10×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/255.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/255.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: مَنْ قَالَ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ، وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ عَشْرَ مِرَارٍ، كَانَ كَمَنْ أَعْتَقَ أَرْبَعَةَ أَنْفُسٍ مِنْ وَلَدِ إِسْمَاعِيلَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Abu Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said: ‘Whoever says: (La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shayin qadeer.) a hundred times during the day, has the reward of freeing ten slaves, a hundred good deeds are recorded for him and a hundred bad deeds are wiped away and he has gained refuge from the devil that day until evening and none shall come with anything better except someone who has done more.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Abu Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said: ‘Whoever says: (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner. To Him belongs all sovereignty and praise and He is over all things omnipotent.) a hundred times during the day, has the reward of freeing ten slaves, a hundred good deeds are recorded for him and a hundred bad deeds are wiped away and he has gained refuge from the devil that day until evening and none shall come with anything better except someone who has done more.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-256`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/256.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/256.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: كَلِمَتَانِ خَفِيفَتَانِ عَلَى اللِّسَانِ، ثَقِيلَتَانِ فِي الْمِيزَانِ، حَبِيبَتَانِ إِلَى الرَّحْمَنِ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ، سُبْحانَ اللَّهِ الْعَظِيمِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Aboo Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said:’(There are) Two words, (which are ) light on the tongue, heavy on the Scale and beloved to The Most Gracious: (Subhanal-lahi wabihamdih, wasubhanal-lahil-'atheem.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Aboo Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said:’(There are) Two words, (which are ) light on the tongue, heavy on the Scale and beloved to The Most Gracious: (How perfect Allah is and I praise Him. How perfect Allah is, The Supreme.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>

#### 4. `hisn-257`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/257.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/257.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: لَأَنْ أَقُولَ سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَلاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، أَحَبُّ إِلَيَّ مِمَّا طَلَعَتْ عَلَيْهِ الشَّمسُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Aboo Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said: ‘Saying: (Subhanal-lah, walhamdu lillah, la ilaha illal-lah wallahu akbar.) is more beloved to me than everything the sun has risen over.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Aboo Hurayrah  reported that the Messenger of Allah  said: ‘Saying: (How perfect Allah is, and all praise is for Allah. None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, and Allah is the greatest.) is more beloved to me than everything the sun has risen over.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>

#### 5. `hisn-258`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 100×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/258.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/258.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">وَقَالَ صلى الله عليه وسلم: أَيَعْجِزُ أَحَدُكُم أَنْ يَكْسِبَ كُلَّ يَوْمٍ أَلْفَ حَسَنَةٍ فَسَأَلَهُ سَائِلٌ مِنْ جُلَسَائِهِ كَيْفَ يَكْسِبُ أَحَدُنَا أَلْفَ حَسَنَةٍ؟ قَالَ: يُسَبِّحُ مِائَةَ تَسْبِيحَةٍ، فَيُكتَبُ لَهُ أَلْفُ حَسَنَةٍ أَوْ يُحَطُّ عَنْهُ أَلْفُ خَطِيئَةٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Sa’d  said: ‘We were sitting with the Messenger of Allah , and he said: ‘Are any of you unable to gain a thousand good deeds each day?’ Somebody then asked him  : How does one achieve a thousand good deeds? He replied: ‘He should say: (Subhanal-lah.) one hundred times, for a thousand good deeds are recorded for him or a thousand bad deeds are wiped away.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Sa’d  said: ‘We were sitting with the Messenger of Allah , and he said: ‘Are any of you unable to gain a thousand good deeds each day?’ Somebody then asked him  : How does one achieve a thousand good deeds? He replied: ‘He should say: (How perfect Allah is.) one hundred times, for a thousand good deeds are recorded for him or a thousand bad deeds are wiped away.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>

> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah

#### 9. `hisn-262`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/262.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/262.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">جَاءَ أَعْرَابِيٌّ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ: عَلِّمْنِي كَلاماً أَقُولُهُ: قَالَ: قُلْ: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ كَبِيراً، وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ كَثِيراً، سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ رَبِّ العَالَمِينَ، لاَ حَوْلَ وَلاَ قُوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللَّهِ الْعَزِيزِ الْحَكِيمِ قَالَ: فَهَؤُلاَءِ لِرَبِّي، فَمَا لِي؟ قَالَ: قُلْ: اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي، وَارْحَمْنِي، وَاهْدِنِي، وَارْزُقْنِي.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Sa'd Ibn Abee Waqqas  narrated that a man came to the Messenger of Allah  and said to him: ‘Teach me something which I should say?’ He said: ‘Say: (La ilaha illal-lah, wahdahu la shareeka lah, Allahu akbaru kabeera, walhamdu lillahi katheera, subhanal-lahi rabbil-'alameen, la hawla wala quwwata illa billahil-'azeezil-hakeem.) the man then said: ‘These are for my Lord, and what is for me?’ He  replied: ‘Say:(Allahummagh-fir lee, warhamnee, wahdinee, warzuqnee.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (SaAAd Ibn Abee Waqqas  narrated that a man came to the Messenger of Allah  and said to him: ‘Teach me something which I should say?’ He said: ‘Say: (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone without partener. Allah is most great and much praise is for Allah. How perfect Allah is, Lord of the worlds. There is no might nor power except with Allah, The Exalted in might, The Wise.) the man then said: ‘These are for my Lord, and what is for me?’ He  replied: ‘Say:(O Allah, forgive me, have mercy upon me, guide me and grant me sustenance.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>  

---

#### 10. `hisn-263`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/263.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/263.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">كَانَ الرَّجُلُ إِذَا أَسْلَمَ عَلَّمَهُ النَّبيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم الصَّلاَةَ ثُمَّ أَمَرَهُ أَنْ يَدْعُوَ بِهَؤُلاَءِ الْكَلِمَاتِ: اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرِ لِي، وَارْحَمْنِي، وَاهْدِنِي، وَعَافِنِي وَارْزُقْنِي.</span>

> (Tariq Al-AshjaAAee  said: ‘When someone would embrace Islam, the Prophet  would teach him how to perform prayer and then order him to supplicate with the following words: (O Allah, forgive me, have mercy upon me, guide me, give me health and grant me sustenance.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The excellence of remembering Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل التسبيح و التحميد، و التهليل، و التكبير</span>

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِنَّ أَفْضَلَ الدُّعَاءِ الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ، وَأَفْضَلَ الذِّكْرِ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ.</span>

### How the Prophet ﷺ glorified Allah

#### 1. `hisn-266`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/266.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/266.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو رضي الله عنه قَالَ: رَأَيْتُ النَّبيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَعْقِدُ التَّسْبِيحَ وفي زيادةٍ: بِيَمِينِهِ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Tasbeeh, it means here, to say: (Subhanal-lah, alhamdu lillah, Allahu akbar. (AAabdullah Ibn AAamr  said: ’I saw the prophet  make tasbeeh with his right hand.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — How the Prophet ﷺ glorified Allah  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — كيف كان النبي يسبح؟</span>

### Goodness and good manners

#### 1. `hisn-267`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/267.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/267.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا كَانَ جُنْحُ اللَّيْلِ – أَوْ أَمْسَيْتُم – فَـكُفُّوا صِبْيانَـكُم، فَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ تَنْتَشِرُ حِينَئِذٍ، فَإِذَا ذَهَبَ سَاعَةٌ مِنَ اللَّيلِ فَخَلُّوهُمْ، وَأَغْلِقُوا الأَبْوَابَ وَاذْكُرُوا اسْمَ اللَّهِ؛ فَإِنَّ الشَّيطَانَ لاَ يَفْتَحُ بَاباً مُغلَقاً، وَأَوْكُوا قِرَبَكُمْ، وَاذْكُرُوا اسْمَ اللَّهِ، وَخَمِّرُوا آنِيَتَكُم، وَاذْكُرُوا اسْمَ اللَّهِ، وَلَوْ أَنْ تَعْرُضُوا عَلَيْهَا شَيْئاً، وَأَطْفِئُوا مَصَابِيحَكُمْ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> When evening descends, bring your children indoors for the devils scatter out at this hour. Then after the passing of an hour (i.e. the first hour) of the night, (you may) let them (the children) go. Close your doors while mentioning the Name of Allah, for the devil may not open a closed door. Fasten your waterskins mentioning the Name of Allah. Cover your eating vessels mentioning the Name of Allah even if you just put something over it, and extinguish your lamps. Peace and blessing be upon our Prophet, Muhammad, and upon his family and his Companions, all of them.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Goodness and good manners  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — من أنواع الخير والآداب الجامعة</span>  

---


## Prayers of the Prophets
<a id="prophets"></a>

_In the Qur'an's own words._

### Adam

<sub>The first supplication ever made by a human being, and the pattern for every one since: name the wrong, ask for the mercy.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-adam-zalamna-anfusana`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا ظَلَمۡنَآ أَنفُسَنَا وَإِن لَّمۡ تَغۡفِرۡ لَنَا وَتَرۡحَمۡنَا لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ ٱلۡخَٰسِرِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, we have wronged ourselves, and if You do not forgive us and have mercy upon us, we will surely be among the losers  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-A'raf 7:23  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأعراف 7:23</span>  

---

### Nuh

<sub>Nine hundred and fifty years of calling. These are the words of the man at the end of them.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-nuh-inni-maghlub`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَنِّي مَغۡلُوبٞ فَٱنتَصِرۡ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Indeed, I am overpowered, so help  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Qamar 54:10  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة القمر 54:10</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-nuh-munzalan-mubarakan`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَّبِّ أَنزِلۡنِي مُنزَلٗا مُّبَارَكٗا وَأَنتَ خَيۡرُ ٱلۡمُنزِلِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, let me land at a blessed landing place, and You are the best to accommodate [us]  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Mu'minun 23:29  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة المؤمنون 23:29</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-nuh-ighfir-li-wa-liwalidayya`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَّبِّ ٱغۡفِرۡ لِي وَلِوَٰلِدَيَّ وَلِمَن دَخَلَ بَيۡتِيَ مُؤۡمِنٗا وَلِلۡمُؤۡمِنِينَ وَٱلۡمُؤۡمِنَٰتِۖ وَلَا تَزِدِ ٱلظَّـٰلِمِينَ إِلَّا تَبَارَۢا</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, forgive me and my parents and whoever enters my house a believer and the believing men and believing women. And do not increase the wrongdoers except in destruction  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Nuh 71:28  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة نوح 71:28</span>  

---

### Ibrahim

<sub>More of the Qur'an's supplications belong to Ibrahim than to any other prophet before Muhammad ﷺ. Several are said in the prayer to this day.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-ibrahim-rabbi-hab-li-hukman`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ هَبۡ لِي حُكۡمٗا وَأَلۡحِقۡنِي بِٱلصَّـٰلِحِينَ وَٱجۡعَل لِّي لِسَانَ صِدۡقٖ فِي ٱلۡأٓخِرِينَ وَٱجۡعَلۡنِي مِن وَرَثَةِ جَنَّةِ ٱلنَّعِيمِ وَٱغۡفِرۡ لِأَبِيٓ إِنَّهُۥ كَانَ مِنَ ٱلضَّآلِّينَ وَلَا تُخۡزِنِي يَوۡمَ يُبۡعَثُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> [And he said], "My Lord, grant me authority and join me with the righteous And grant me a reputation of honor among later generations And place me among the inheritors of the Garden of Pleasure And forgive my father. Indeed, he has been of those astray And do not disgrace me on the Day they are [all] resurrected  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ash-Shu'ara 26:83-87  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الشعراء 26:83-87</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-ibrahim-taqabbal-minna`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا تَقَبَّلۡ مِنَّآۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلسَّمِيعُ ٱلۡعَلِيمُ رَبَّنَا وَٱجۡعَلۡنَا مُسۡلِمَيۡنِ لَكَ وَمِن ذُرِّيَّتِنَآ أُمَّةٗ مُّسۡلِمَةٗ لَّكَ وَأَرِنَا مَنَاسِكَنَا وَتُبۡ عَلَيۡنَآۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ رَبَّنَا وَٱبۡعَثۡ فِيهِمۡ رَسُولٗا مِّنۡهُمۡ يَتۡلُواْ عَلَيۡهِمۡ ءَايَٰتِكَ وَيُعَلِّمُهُمُ ٱلۡكِتَٰبَ وَٱلۡحِكۡمَةَ وَيُزَكِّيهِمۡۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلۡعَزِيزُ ٱلۡحَكِيمُ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, accept [this] from us. Indeed You are the Hearing, the Knowing Our Lord, and make us Muslims [in submission] to You and from our descendants a Muslim nation [in submission] to You. And show us our rites and accept our repentance. Indeed, You are the Accepting of repentance, the Merciful Our Lord, and send among them a messenger from themselves who will recite to them Your verses and teach them the Book and wisdom and purify them. Indeed, You are the Exalted in Might, the Wise  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Baqarah 2:127-129  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة البقرة 2:127-129</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-ibrahim-baladan-aminan`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱجۡعَلۡ هَٰذَا ٱلۡبَلَدَ ءَامِنٗا وَٱجۡنُبۡنِي وَبَنِيَّ أَن نَّعۡبُدَ ٱلۡأَصۡنَامَ رَبِّ إِنَّهُنَّ أَضۡلَلۡنَ كَثِيرٗا مِّنَ ٱلنَّاسِۖ فَمَن تَبِعَنِي فَإِنَّهُۥ مِنِّيۖ وَمَنۡ عَصَانِي فَإِنَّكَ غَفُورٞ رَّحِيمٞ رَّبَّنَآ إِنِّيٓ أَسۡكَنتُ مِن ذُرِّيَّتِي بِوَادٍ غَيۡرِ ذِي زَرۡعٍ عِندَ بَيۡتِكَ ٱلۡمُحَرَّمِ رَبَّنَا لِيُقِيمُواْ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ فَٱجۡعَلۡ أَفۡـِٔدَةٗ مِّنَ ٱلنَّاسِ تَهۡوِيٓ إِلَيۡهِمۡ وَٱرۡزُقۡهُم مِّنَ ٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ لَعَلَّهُمۡ يَشۡكُرُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, make this city [Makkah] secure and keep me and my sons away from worshipping idols My Lord, indeed they have led astray many among the people. So whoever follows me - then he is of me; and whoever disobeys me - indeed, You are [yet] Forgiving and Merciful Our Lord, I have settled some of my descendants in an uncultivated valley near Your sacred House, our Lord, that they may establish prayer. So make hearts among the people incline toward them and provide for them from the fruits that they might be grateful  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ibrahim 14:35-37  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة ابراهيم 14:35-37</span>  

---

#### 4. `dua-ibrahim-muqim-as-salah`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱجۡعَلۡنِي مُقِيمَ ٱلصَّلَوٰةِ وَمِن ذُرِّيَّتِيۚ رَبَّنَا وَتَقَبَّلۡ دُعَآءِ رَبَّنَا ٱغۡفِرۡ لِي وَلِوَٰلِدَيَّ وَلِلۡمُؤۡمِنِينَ يَوۡمَ يَقُومُ ٱلۡحِسَابُ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, make me an establisher of prayer, and [many] from my descendants. Our Lord, and accept my supplication Our Lord, forgive me and my parents and the believers the Day the account is established  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ibrahim 14:40-41  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة ابراهيم 14:40-41</span>  

---

### Lut and Shu'ayb

<sub>Two prophets whose people would not hear them, and what each asked for at the end.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-lut-najjini-wa-ahli`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ نَجِّنِي وَأَهۡلِي مِمَّا يَعۡمَلُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, save me and my family from [the consequence of] what they do  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ash-Shu'ara 26:169  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الشعراء 26:169</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-lut-unsurni`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱنصُرۡنِي عَلَى ٱلۡقَوۡمِ ٱلۡمُفۡسِدِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, support me against the corrupting people  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-'Ankabut 29:30  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة العنكبوت 29:30</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-shuayb-iftah-baynana`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا ٱفۡتَحۡ بَيۡنَنَا وَبَيۡنَ قَوۡمِنَا بِٱلۡحَقِّ وَأَنتَ خَيۡرُ ٱلۡفَٰتِحِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, decide between us and our people in truth, and You are the best of those who give decision  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-A'raf 7:89  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأعراف 7:89</span>  

---

### Yusuf and Ya'qub

<sub>A father who lost a son and a son who lost everything. Neither complained to anyone but God.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-yusuf-as-sijnu-ahabbu`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱلسِّجۡنُ أَحَبُّ إِلَيَّ مِمَّا يَدۡعُونَنِيٓ إِلَيۡهِۖ وَإِلَّا تَصۡرِفۡ عَنِّي كَيۡدَهُنَّ أَصۡبُ إِلَيۡهِنَّ وَأَكُن مِّنَ ٱلۡجَٰهِلِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, prison is more to my liking than that to which they invite me. And if You do not avert from me their plan, I might incline toward them and [thus] be of the ignorant  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Yusuf 12:33  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة يوسف 12:33</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-yusuf-tawaffani-musliman`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ قَدۡ ءَاتَيۡتَنِي مِنَ ٱلۡمُلۡكِ وَعَلَّمۡتَنِي مِن تَأۡوِيلِ ٱلۡأَحَادِيثِۚ فَاطِرَ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلۡأَرۡضِ أَنتَ وَلِيِّۦ فِي ٱلدُّنۡيَا وَٱلۡأٓخِرَةِۖ تَوَفَّنِي مُسۡلِمٗا وَأَلۡحِقۡنِي بِٱلصَّـٰلِحِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, You have given me [something] of sovereignty and taught me of the interpretation of dreams. Creator of the heavens and earth, You are my protector in this world and in the Hereafter. Cause me to die a Muslim and join me with the righteous  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Yusuf 12:101  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة يوسف 12:101</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-yaqub-ashku-bathi`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِنَّمَآ أَشۡكُواْ بَثِّي وَحُزۡنِيٓ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ وَأَعۡلَمُ مِنَ ٱللَّهِ مَا لَا تَعۡلَمُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> I only complain of my suffering and my grief to Allah, and I know from Allah that which you do not know  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Yusuf 12:86  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة يوسف 12:86</span>  

---

### Musa

<sub>Musa's supplications are the shortest in the Qur'an and the most quoted. Three of them are said before speaking in public to this day.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-musa-zalamtu-nafsi`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ إِنِّي ظَلَمۡتُ نَفۡسِي فَٱغۡفِرۡ لِي</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, indeed I have wronged myself, so forgive me,  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Qasas 28:16  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة القصص 28:16</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-musa-faqir`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ إِنِّي لِمَآ أَنزَلۡتَ إِلَيَّ مِنۡ خَيۡرٖ فَقِيرٞ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, indeed I am, for whatever good You would send down to me, in need  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Qasas 28:24  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة القصص 28:24</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-musa-ishrah-li-sadri`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱشۡرَحۡ لِي صَدۡرِي وَيَسِّرۡ لِيٓ أَمۡرِي وَٱحۡلُلۡ عُقۡدَةٗ مِّن لِّسَانِي يَفۡقَهُواْ قَوۡلِي</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, expand for me my breast [with assurance] And ease for me my task And untie the knot from my tongue That they may understand my speech  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Taha 20:25-28  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة طه 20:25-28</span>  

---

#### 4. `dua-musa-ighfir-li-wa-liakhi`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱغۡفِرۡ لِي وَلِأَخِي وَأَدۡخِلۡنَا فِي رَحۡمَتِكَۖ وَأَنتَ أَرۡحَمُ ٱلرَّـٰحِمِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, forgive me and my brother and admit us into Your mercy, for You are the most merciful of the merciful  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-A'raf 7:151  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأعراف 7:151</span>  

---

### Ayyub and Yunus

<sub>The two supplications of last resort. The Prophet ﷺ said of Yunus's words that no Muslim ever supplicates with them without being answered.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-ayyub-massaniya-ad-durr`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَنِّي مَسَّنِيَ ٱلضُّرُّ وَأَنتَ أَرۡحَمُ ٱلرَّـٰحِمِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Indeed, adversity has touched me, and you are the Most Merciful of the merciful  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Anbya 21:83  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأنبياء 21:83</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-yunus-la-ilaha-illa-anta`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لَّآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّآ أَنتَ سُبۡحَٰنَكَ إِنِّي كُنتُ مِنَ ٱلظَّـٰلِمِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> There is no deity except You; exalted are You. Indeed, I have been of the wrongdoers  

**Virtue**
> The Prophet ﷺ said: "No Muslim supplicates with it for anything, ever, but Allah answers him."  

**Virtue (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">قال النبي ﷺ: «لم يدعُ بها رجلٌ مسلمٌ في شيءٍ قط إلا استجاب الله له».</span>  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Anbya 21:87  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأنبياء 21:87</span>  

---

### Dawud and Sulayman

<sub>Two kings. Both asked for something other than the kingdom.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-sulayman-awzini-an-ashkura`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ أَوۡزِعۡنِيٓ أَنۡ أَشۡكُرَ نِعۡمَتَكَ ٱلَّتِيٓ أَنۡعَمۡتَ عَلَيَّ وَعَلَىٰ وَٰلِدَيَّ وَأَنۡ أَعۡمَلَ صَٰلِحٗا تَرۡضَىٰهُ وَأَدۡخِلۡنِي بِرَحۡمَتِكَ فِي عِبَادِكَ ٱلصَّـٰلِحِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, enable me to be grateful for Your favor which You have bestowed upon me and upon my parents and to do righteousness of which You approve. And admit me by Your mercy into [the ranks of] Your righteous servants  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat An-Naml 27:19  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة النمل 27:19</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-sulayman-mulkan`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ ٱغۡفِرۡ لِي وَهَبۡ لِي مُلۡكٗا لَّا يَنۢبَغِي لِأَحَدٖ مِّنۢ بَعۡدِيٓۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلۡوَهَّابُ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, forgive me and grant me a kingdom such as will not belong to anyone after me. Indeed, You are the Bestower  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Sad 38:35  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة ص 38:35</span>  

---

### Zakariyya and 'Isa

<sub>An old man asking for a child, and the child's contemporary asking for a table.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-zakariyya-dhurriyyatan-tayyibah`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ هَبۡ لِي مِن لَّدُنكَ ذُرِّيَّةٗ طَيِّبَةًۖ إِنَّكَ سَمِيعُ ٱلدُّعَآءِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, grant me from Yourself a good offspring. Indeed, You are the Hearer of supplication  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ali 'Imran 3:38  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة آل عمران 3:38</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-zakariyya-la-tadharni-fardan`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبِّ لَا تَذَرۡنِي فَرۡدٗا وَأَنتَ خَيۡرُ ٱلۡوَٰرِثِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, do not leave me alone [with no heir], while you are the best of inheritors  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Anbya 21:89  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الأنبياء 21:89</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-isa-anzil-alayna-maidah`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">ٱللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَآ أَنزِلۡ عَلَيۡنَا مَآئِدَةٗ مِّنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ تَكُونُ لَنَا عِيدٗا لِّأَوَّلِنَا وَءَاخِرِنَا وَءَايَةٗ مِّنكَۖ وَٱرۡزُقۡنَا وَأَنتَ خَيۡرُ ٱلرَّـٰزِقِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, our Lord, send down to us a table [spread with food] from the heaven to be for us a festival for the first of us and the last of us and a sign from You. And provide for us, and You are the best of providers  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Ma'idah 5:114  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة المائدة 5:114</span>  

---

### Muhammad ﷺ, in the Qur'an

<sub>The supplications God taught His Messenger ﷺ directly, in the revelation itself.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-nabi-rabbi-zidni-ilman`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَّبِّ زِدۡنِي عِلۡمٗا</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, increase me in knowledge  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Taha 20:114  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة طه 20:114</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-nabi-mudkhala-sidqin`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَّبِّ أَدۡخِلۡنِي مُدۡخَلَ صِدۡقٖ وَأَخۡرِجۡنِي مُخۡرَجَ صِدۡقٖ وَٱجۡعَل لِّي مِن لَّدُنكَ سُلۡطَٰنٗا نَّصِيرٗا</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, cause me to enter a sound entrance and to exit a sound exit and grant me from Yourself a supporting authority  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Isra 17:80  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الإسراء 17:80</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-nabi-rabbi-ighfir-warham`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَّبِّ ٱغۡفِرۡ وَٱرۡحَمۡ وَأَنتَ خَيۡرُ ٱلرَّـٰحِمِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> My Lord, forgive and have mercy, and You are the best of the merciful  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Mu'minun 23:118  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة المؤمنون 23:118</span>  

---

### The prayers the Qur'an teaches

<sub>Not tied to one prophet: the supplications the Qur'an puts in the mouths of the believers, for anyone to take up. Read straight through, they are a complete du'a.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-rabbana-atina-fid-dunya`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَآ ءَاتِنَا فِي ٱلدُّنۡيَا حَسَنَةٗ وَفِي ٱلۡأٓخِرَةِ حَسَنَةٗ وَقِنَا عَذَابَ ٱلنَّارِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, give us in this world [that which is] good and in the Hereafter [that which is] good and protect us from the punishment of the Fire  

**Virtue**
> Anas ibn Malik said that the supplication the Prophet ﷺ made most often was this one.  

**Virtue (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">قال أنس: كان أكثر دعاء النبي ﷺ هذا.</span>  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Baqarah 2:201  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة البقرة 2:201</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-rabbana-la-tuakhidhna`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا لَا تُؤَاخِذۡنَآ إِن نَّسِينَآ أَوۡ أَخۡطَأۡنَاۚ رَبَّنَا وَلَا تَحۡمِلۡ عَلَيۡنَآ إِصۡرٗا كَمَا حَمَلۡتَهُۥ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبۡلِنَاۚ رَبَّنَا وَلَا تُحَمِّلۡنَا مَا لَا طَاقَةَ لَنَا بِهِۦۖ وَٱعۡفُ عَنَّا وَٱغۡفِرۡ لَنَا وَٱرۡحَمۡنَآۚ أَنتَ مَوۡلَىٰنَا فَٱنصُرۡنَا عَلَى ٱلۡقَوۡمِ ٱلۡكَٰفِرِينَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, do not impose blame upon us if we have forgotten or erred. Our Lord, and lay not upon us a burden like that which You laid upon those before us. Our Lord, and burden us not with that which we have no ability to bear. And pardon us; and forgive us; and have mercy upon us. You are our protector, so give us victory over the disbelieving people  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Baqarah 2:286  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة البقرة 2:286</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-rabbana-la-tuzigh-qulubana`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا لَا تُزِغۡ قُلُوبَنَا بَعۡدَ إِذۡ هَدَيۡتَنَا وَهَبۡ لَنَا مِن لَّدُنكَ رَحۡمَةًۚ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلۡوَهَّابُ رَبَّنَآ إِنَّكَ جَامِعُ ٱلنَّاسِ لِيَوۡمٖ لَّا رَيۡبَ فِيهِۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَا يُخۡلِفُ ٱلۡمِيعَادَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> [Who say], "Our Lord, let not our hearts deviate after You have guided us and grant us from Yourself mercy. Indeed, You are the Bestower Our Lord, surely You will gather the people for a Day about which there is no doubt. Indeed, Allah does not fail in His promise  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ali 'Imran 3:8-9  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة آل عمران 3:8-9</span>  

---

#### 4. `dua-rabbana-innana-amanna`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَآ إِنَّنَآ ءَامَنَّا فَٱغۡفِرۡ لَنَا ذُنُوبَنَا وَقِنَا عَذَابَ ٱلنَّارِ ٱلصَّـٰبِرِينَ وَٱلصَّـٰدِقِينَ وَٱلۡقَٰنِتِينَ وَٱلۡمُنفِقِينَ وَٱلۡمُسۡتَغۡفِرِينَ بِٱلۡأَسۡحَارِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, indeed we have believed, so forgive us our sins and protect us from the punishment of the Fire The patient, the true, the obedient, those who spend [in the way of Allah], and those who seek forgiveness before dawn  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ali 'Imran 3:16-17  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة آل عمران 3:16-17</span>  

---

#### 5. `dua-rabbana-ma-khalaqta-hadha-batilan`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا مَا خَلَقۡتَ هَٰذَا بَٰطِلٗا سُبۡحَٰنَكَ فَقِنَا عَذَابَ ٱلنَّارِ رَبَّنَآ إِنَّكَ مَن تُدۡخِلِ ٱلنَّارَ فَقَدۡ أَخۡزَيۡتَهُۥۖ وَمَا لِلظَّـٰلِمِينَ مِنۡ أَنصَارٖ رَّبَّنَآ إِنَّنَا سَمِعۡنَا مُنَادِيٗا يُنَادِي لِلۡإِيمَٰنِ أَنۡ ءَامِنُواْ بِرَبِّكُمۡ فَـَٔامَنَّاۚ رَبَّنَا فَٱغۡفِرۡ لَنَا ذُنُوبَنَا وَكَفِّرۡ عَنَّا سَيِّـَٔاتِنَا وَتَوَفَّنَا مَعَ ٱلۡأَبۡرَارِ رَبَّنَا وَءَاتِنَا مَا وَعَدتَّنَا عَلَىٰ رُسُلِكَ وَلَا تُخۡزِنَا يَوۡمَ ٱلۡقِيَٰمَةِۖ إِنَّكَ لَا تُخۡلِفُ ٱلۡمِيعَادَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, You did not create this aimlessly; exalted are You [above such a thing]; then protect us from the punishment of the Fire Our Lord, indeed whoever You admit to the Fire - You have disgraced him, and for the wrongdoers there are no helpers Our Lord, indeed we have heard a caller calling to faith, [saying], 'Believe in your Lord,' and we have believed. Our Lord, so forgive us our sins and remove from us our misdeeds and cause us to die with the righteous Our Lord, and grant us what You promised us through Your messengers and do not disgrace us on the Day of Resurrection. Indeed, You do not fail in [Your] promise  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Ali 'Imran 3:191-194  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة آل عمران 3:191-194</span>  

---

#### 6. `dua-rabbana-hab-lana-qurrata-ayun`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا هَبۡ لَنَا مِنۡ أَزۡوَٰجِنَا وَذُرِّيَّـٰتِنَا قُرَّةَ أَعۡيُنٖ وَٱجۡعَلۡنَا لِلۡمُتَّقِينَ إِمَامًا</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, grant us from among our wives and offspring comfort to our eyes and make us an example for the righteous  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Furqan 25:74  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الفرقان 25:74</span>  

---

#### 7. `dua-rabbana-atmim-lana-nurana`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَآ أَتۡمِمۡ لَنَا نُورَنَا وَٱغۡفِرۡ لَنَآۖ إِنَّكَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَيۡءٖ قَدِيرٞ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, perfect for us our light and forgive us. Indeed, You are over all things competent  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat At-Tahrim 66:8  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة التحريم 66:8</span>  

---

#### 8. `dua-rabbana-ighfir-lana-wa-liikhwanina`

**Attribution**: Qur'an  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">رَبَّنَا ٱغۡفِرۡ لَنَا وَلِإِخۡوَٰنِنَا ٱلَّذِينَ سَبَقُونَا بِٱلۡإِيمَٰنِ وَلَا تَجۡعَلۡ فِي قُلُوبِنَا غِلّٗا لِّلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُواْ رَبَّنَآ إِنَّكَ رَءُوفٞ رَّحِيمٌ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> Our Lord, forgive us and our brothers who preceded us in faith and put not in our hearts [any] resentment toward those who have believed. Our Lord, indeed You are Kind and Merciful  

**Reference**
> The Qur'an — Surat Al-Hashr 59:10  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">القرآن — سورة الحشر 59:10</span>  

---


## Prayers from the Seerah
<a id="seerah"></a>

_Said at a moment in the Prophet's ﷺ life._

### The years in Makkah

<sub>Thirteen years of being disbelieved, and what he asked for during them.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-seerah-ighfir-li-qawmi`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِقَوْمِي فَإِنَّهُمْ لاَ يَعْلَمُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, forgive my people, for they do not know.  

**Reference**
> Sahih al-Bukhari 3477 (Book 60, no. 3331). Narrated by 'Abdullah ibn Mas'ud.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح البخاري، عن عبد الله بن مسعود رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-seerah-aizz-al-islam`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَعِزَّ الإِسْلاَمَ بِأَحَبِّ هَذَيْنِ الرَّجُلَيْنِ إِلَيْكَ بِأَبِي جَهْلٍ أَوْ بِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, strengthen Islam with whichever of these two men is more beloved to You — with Abu Jahl, or with 'Umar ibn al-Khattab.  

**Reference**
> Jami' at-Tirmidhi 3681 (no. 3774), narrated by Ibn 'Umar. Graded hasan sahih gharib by at-Tirmidhi.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن الترمذي، عن ابن عمر، قال الترمذي: حسن صحيح غريب.</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-seerah-ya-muqallib`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يَا مُقَلِّبَ الْقُلُوبِ ثَبِّتْ قَلْبِي عَلَى دِينِكَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Turner of hearts, make my heart firm upon Your religion.  

**Reference**
> Jami' at-Tirmidhi 3522 (no. 3606), narrated by Umm Salamah. Graded hasan by at-Tirmidhi. Also narrated from Anas at no. 2208.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن الترمذي، عن أم سلمة، قال الترمذي: حسن.</span>  

---

### Badr

<sub>Three hundred and nineteen men against a thousand.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-seerah-badr`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَنْجِزْ لِي مَا وَعَدْتَنِي اللَّهُمَّ آتِ مَا وَعَدْتَنِي اللَّهُمَّ إِنْ تَهْلِكْ هَذِهِ الْعِصَابَةُ مِنْ أَهْلِ الإِسْلاَمِ لاَ تُعْبَدْ فِي الأَرْضِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, fulfil for me what You promised me. O Allah, bring about what You promised me. O Allah, if this band of the people of Islam perishes, You will not be worshipped on the earth.  

**Reference**
> Sahih Muslim 1763 (no. 4456), narrated by 'Umar ibn al-Khattab.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح مسلم (١٧٦٣)، عن عمر بن الخطاب رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---

### Madinah

<sub>They arrived in a place that was making them ill, having left the place they loved.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-seerah-madinah`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ حَبِّبْ إِلَيْنَا الْمَدِينَةَ كَحُبِّنَا مَكَّةَ أَوْ أَشَدَّ، اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِي صَاعِنَا، وَفِي مُدِّنَا، وَصَحِّحْهَا لَنَا وَانْقُلْ حُمَّاهَا إِلَى الْجُحْفَةِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, make Madinah beloved to us as we love Makkah, or more. O Allah, bless us in our sa' and in our mudd, and make it healthy for us, and move its fever to al-Juhfah.  

**Reference**
> Sahih al-Bukhari 1889 (no. 1821), narrated by 'A'ishah.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح البخاري، عن عائشة رضي الله عنها.</span>  

---

### The day his son died

<sub>Not a supplication so much as permission — what a believer is allowed to feel.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-seerah-ibrahim`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِنَّ الْعَيْنَ تَدْمَعُ، وَالْقَلْبَ يَحْزَنُ، وَلاَ نَقُولُ إِلاَّ مَا يَرْضَى رَبُّنَا، وَإِنَّا بِفِرَاقِكَ يَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ لَمَحْزُونُونَ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> The eye sheds tears and the heart grieves, and we say nothing but what pleases our Lord. And we are grieved by your parting, Ibrahim.  

**Reference**
> Sahih al-Bukhari 1303 (no. 1261), narrated by Anas ibn Malik.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح البخاري، عن أنس بن مالك رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---


## Prayers of the Companions
<a id="companions"></a>

_Taught to them, made for them, said by them._

### Taught to one of them

<sub>Someone asked him what to say. This is what he answered.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-abu-bakr-fi-salati`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي ظَلَمْتُ نَفْسِي ظُلْمًا كَثِيرًا وَلاَ يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ فَاغْفِرْ لِي مَغْفِرَةً مِنْ عِنْدِكَ وَارْحَمْنِي إِنَّكَ أَنْتَ الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allahumma innee ẓalamtu nafsee ẓulman katheeran, wa la yaghfirudh-dhunooba illa anta, faghfir lee maghfiratan min 'indik, warḥamnee, innaka antal-Ghafoorur-Raheem.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, I have greatly wronged myself, and none forgives sins but You. So forgive me a forgiveness from Yourself, and have mercy on me. You are the Forgiving, the Merciful.  

**Reference**
> Sunan an-Nasa'i 1304; also Sahih al-Bukhari 834 and Sahih Muslim 2705. Narrated by Abu Bakr as-Siddiq.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن النسائي، والبخاري (٨٣٤)، ومسلم (٢٧٠٥)، عن أبي بكر الصديق رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-aishah-laylat-al-qadr`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّكَ عَفُوٌّ تُحِبُّ الْعَفْوَ فَاعْفُ عَنِّي</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allahumma innaka 'afuwwun tuḥibbul-'afwa fa'fu 'annee.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, You are Pardoning and love to pardon, so pardon me.  

**Reference**
> Sunan Ibn Majah 3850 (no. 3586); also Jami' at-Tirmidhi 3513. Narrated by 'A'ishah. Graded sahih by al-Albani.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن ابن ماجه، والترمذي (٣٥١٣)، عن عائشة رضي الله عنها، وصححه الألباني.</span>  

---

#### 3. `dua-muadh-ainni`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَعِنِّي عَلَى ذِكْرِكَ وَشُكْرِكَ وَحُسْنِ عِبَادَتِكَ</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allahumma a'innee 'ala dhikrika wa shukrika wa ḥusni 'ibadatik.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, help me to remember You, to thank You, and to worship You well.  

**Reference**
> Sunan Abi Dawud 1522 (no. 1523), narrated by Mu'adh ibn Jabal. Graded sahih by al-Albani.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن أبي داود (١٥٢٢)، عن معاذ بن جبل رضي الله عنه، وصححه الألباني.</span>  

---

#### 4. `dua-ali-ihdini`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اهْدِنِي وَسَدِّدْنِي</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allahummah-dinee wa saddidnee.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, guide me and keep me straight.  

**Reference**
> Sunan an-Nasa'i 5221; also Sahih Muslim 2725 and Sunan Abi Dawud 4226. Narrated by 'Ali ibn Abi Talib.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن النسائي، ومسلم (٢٧٢٥)، وأبو داود (٤٢٢٦)، عن علي رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---

#### 5. `dua-ibn-masud-al-huda`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ الْهُدَى وَالتُّقَى وَالْعَفَافَ وَالْغِنَى</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allahumma innee as'alukal-huda wat-tuqa wal-'afafa wal-ghina.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, I ask You for guidance, and piety, and chastity, and sufficiency.  

**Reference**
> Jami' at-Tirmidhi 3489 (no. 3573); also Sahih Muslim 2721. Narrated by 'Abdullah ibn Mas'ud. Graded hasan sahih by at-Tirmidhi.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن الترمذي، ومسلم (٢٧٢١)، عن ابن مسعود رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---

### Prayers he made for them

<sub>Two supplications, and what became of the two people they were made for.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-anas-akthir-malahu`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَكْثِرْ مَالَهُ وَوَلَدَهُ وَبَارِكْ لَهُ فِيمَا أَعْطَيْتَهُ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, increase his wealth and his children, and bless him in what You have given him.  

**Reference**
> Jami' at-Tirmidhi 3833 (no. 3926); also Sahih Muslim 2480 and 660. Narrated by Anas ibn Malik from Umm Sulaym. Graded hasan sahih by at-Tirmidhi.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">سنن الترمذي، ومسلم، عن أنس بن مالك عن أم سليم رضي الله عنهما.</span>  

---

#### 2. `dua-ibn-abbas-faqqihhu`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ فَقِّهْهُ فِي الدِّينِ</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, give him understanding of the religion.  

**Reference**
> Sahih al-Bukhari 143, narrated by Ibn 'Abbas.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح البخاري (١٤٣)، عن ابن عباس رضي الله عنهما.</span>  

---

### Said by one of them

<sub>A companion's own words. Not prophetic — and marked so on the page.</sub>

#### 1. `dua-umar-istisqa`

**Attribution**: Transmitted (from a companion or later)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّا كُنَّا نَتَوَسَّلُ إِلَيْكَ بِنَبِيِّنَا فَتَسْقِينَا وَإِنَّا نَتَوَسَّلُ إِلَيْكَ بِعَمِّ نَبِيِّنَا فَاسْقِنَا</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, we used to ask You by means of our Prophet and You would give us rain. And we ask You now by means of our Prophet's uncle — so give us rain.  

**Reference**
> Sahih al-Bukhari 1010 (no. 982), narrated by Anas ibn Malik.  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">صحيح البخاري (١٠١٠)، عن أنس بن مالك رضي الله عنه.</span>  

---


## When You Need It
<a id="when-you-need-it"></a>

_Grief, fear, debt, illness, loss._

### Worry and grief

#### 1. `hisn-120`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/120.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/120.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي عَبْدُكَ، ابْنُ عَبْدِكَ، ابْنُ أَمَتِكَ، نَاصِيَتِي بِيَدِكَ، مَاضٍ فِيَّ حُكْمُكَ، عَدْلٌ فِيَّ قَضَاؤُكَ، أَسْأَلُكَ بِكُــــلِّ اسْمٍ هُوَ لَكَ، سَمَّيْتَ بِهِ نَفْسَكَ، أَوْ أَنْزَلْتَهُ فِي كِتَابِكَ، أَوْ عَلَّمْتَهُ أَحَداً مِنْ خَلْقِكَ، أَوِ اسْتَأْثَرْتَ بِهِ فِي عِلْمِ الغَيْبِ عِنْدَكَ، أَنْ تَجْعَلَ القُرْآنَ رَبِيعَ قَلْبِي، وَنُورَ صَدْرِي، وَجَلاَءَ حُزْنِي، وَذَهَابَ هَمِّي.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma innee 'abduk, ibnu 'abdik, ibnu amatik, nasiyatee biyadik, madin fiyya hukmuk, 'adlun fiyya qada-ok, as-aluka bikulli ismin huwa lak, sammayta bihi nafsak, aw anzaltahu fee kitabik, aw 'allamtahu ahadan min khalqik awis-ta/tharta bihi fee 'ilmil-ghaybi 'indak, an taj'alal-Qurana rabee'a qalbee, wanoora sadree, wajalaa huznee wathahaba hammee.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, I am Your servant, son of Your servant, son of Your maidservant, my forelock is in Your hand (i.e. You have total mastery over), Your command over me is forever executed and Your decree over me is just. I ask You by every name belonging to You which You named Yourself with, or revealed in Your Book, or You taught to any of Your creation, or You have preserved in the knowledge of the unseen with You, that You make the Quran the life of my heart and the light of my breast, and a departure for my sorrow and a release for my anxiety.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Worry and grief  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الهم والحزن</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-121`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/121.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/121.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنَ الْهَمِّ وَالْحَزَنِ، وَالْعَجْزِ وَالْكَسَلِ، وَالْبُخْلِ وَالْجُبْنِ، وَضَلَعِ الدَّيْنِ وَغَلَبَةِ الرِّجَالِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma innee a'oothu bika minal-hammi walhuzn, wal'ajzi walkasali walbukhli waljubn, wadal'id-dayni waghalabatir-rijal.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, I take refuge in You from anxiety and sorrow, weakness and laziness, miserliness and cowardice, the burden of debts and from being over powered by men.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Worry and grief  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الهم والحزن</span>  

---

### Distress

#### 1. `hisn-122`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/122.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/122.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ الْعَظِيمُ الْحَلِيمُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ رَبُّ الْعَرْشِ الْعَظِيمِ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ رَبُّ السَّمَوَاتِ وَرَبُّ الْأَرْضِ وَرَبُّ الْعَرْشِ الْكَرِيمِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ilaha illal-lahul-'atheemul-haleem, la ilaha illal-lahu rabbul-'arshil-'atheem, la ilaha illal-lahu  rabbus-samawati warabbul-ardi warabbul-'arshil-kareem.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah Forbearing.  None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, Lord of the magnificent throne.  None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, Lord of the heavens, Lord of the Earth and Lord of the noble throne.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Distress  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الكرب</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-123`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/123.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/123.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ رَحْمَتَكَ أَرْجُو، فَلاَ تَكِلْنِي إِلَى نَفْسِي طَرْفَةَ عَيْنٍ، وَأَصْلِحْ لِي شَأْنِي كُلَّهُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma rahmataka arjoo fala takilnee ila nafsee tarfata 'ayn, wa-aslih lee sha/nee kullah, la ilaha illa ant.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, it is Your mercy that I hope for, so do not leave me in charge of my affairs even for a blink of an eye and rectify for me all of my affairs.None has the right to be worshipped except You.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Distress  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الكرب</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-124`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/124.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/124.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ سُبْحَانَكَ إِنِّي كُنْتُ مِنَ الظّالِمِينَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ilaha illa anta subhanaka innee kuntu minath-thalimeen.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except You.  How perfect You are, verily I was among the wrong-doers.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Distress  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الكرب</span>  

---

#### 4. `hisn-125`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/125.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/125.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُ اللَّهُ رَبِّي لاَ أُشْرِكُ بِهِ شَيْئاً.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except You.  How perfect You are, verily I was among the wrong-doers.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Allah, Allah is my Lord, I do not associate anything with Him.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Distress  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الكرب</span>

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)

> (Allahummak-fineehim bima shi/.)

### Against an enemy

#### 1. `hisn-131`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/131.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/131.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ مُنْزِلَ الْكِتَابِ، سَرِيعَ الْحِسَابِ، اهْزِمِ الأَحْزَابَ، اللَّهُمَّ اهزِمْهُمْ وَزَلْزِلْهُمْ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma munzilal-kitab, saree'al-hisab, ihzimil-ahzab, allahummah-zimhum wazalzilhum.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, Revealer of the Book, Swift at reckoning, defeat the confederates. O Allah, defeat them and convulse them.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Against an enemy  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء على العدو</span>

### Debt

#### 1. `hisn-136`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/136.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/136.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اكْفِنِي بِحَلاَلِكَ عَنْ حَرَامِكَ، وَأَغْنِنِي بِفَضْلِكِ عَمَّنْ سِوَاكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummak-finee bihalalika 'an haramik, wa-aghninee bifadlika 'amman siwak.)

> (O Allah, make what is lawful enough for me, as opposed to what is unlawful, and spare me by Your grace, of need of others.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Debt  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء قضاء الدين</span>

### When something becomes difficult

#### 1. `hisn-139`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/139.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/139.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ لاَ سَهْلَ إِلاَّ مَا جَعَلْتَهُ سَهْلاً، وَأَنْتَ تَجْعَلُ الْحَزْنَ إِذَا شِئْتَ سَهْلاً.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma la sahla illa ma ja'altahu sahla, wa-anta taj'alul- hazana in shi'ta sahla.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, there is no ease except in that which You have made easy, and You make the difficulty, if You wish, easy.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When something becomes difficult  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من استصعب عليه أمر</span>

### When something goes wrong

#### 1. `hisn-144`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَدَرُ اللَّهُ وَمَا شَاءَ فَعَلَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**

### After committing a sin

#### 1. `hisn-140`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/140.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/140.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">مَا مِنْ عَبْدٍ يُذنِبُ ذَنْباً فَيُحْسِنُ الطُّهُورَ، ثُمَّ يَقُومُ فَيُصَلِّي رَكْعَتَيْنِ، ثُمَّ يَسْتَغْفِرُ اللَّهَ إِلاَّ غَفَرَ اللَّهُ لَهُ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Any servant who commits a sin and as a result, performs ablution, prays two units of prayer (i.e. two rakAAas) and then seeks Allah’s forgiveness, Allah would forgive him.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — After committing a sin  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول ويفعل من أذنب ذنبا</span>

### Whispering during prayer and recitation

#### 1. `hisn-138`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/138.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/138.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَعُوذُ بِاللَّهِ مِنَ الشَّيطَانِ الرَّجِيمِ، وَاتْفُلْ عَلَى يَسَارِكَ (ثلاثاً).</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (othman Ibn Al-AAas  narrated: I said ‘O Messenger of Allah, verily the devil comes between me and my prayer and recitation making me confused’ The Messenger of Allah  replied ‘That is a devil called Khanzab, so if you sense his presence then seek refuge in Allah from him and spit (A form of spitting comprising mainly of air with little spittle) on your left side three times.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Whispering during prayer and recitation  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الوسوسة في الصلاة و القراءة</span>

**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/135.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/135.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">يَقْرَأُ قَوْلَهُ تَعَالَى: ﴿هُوَ الْأوَّلُ وَالْآخِرُ وَالظّاهِرُ وَالْباطِنُ وَهُوَ بِكُلِّ شَيْءٍ عَلِيمٌ﴾.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (He should also recite the following verse: ( Huwa al-awwalu, waal-akhiru, waalththahiru waalbatinu wahuwa bikulli shayin 'aleem))  

**Translation (en)**
> (He should also recite the following verse: (He is The First and The Last, Aththahir and Al-Batin and He knows well all things.)(ththahir: Indicates the greatness of His attributes and the insignificance of every single creation in respect to His greatness and Highness, for He is above all of His creation as regards His essence and attributes.)(Al-Batin: Indicates His awareness and knowledge of all secrets, of that which is in the hearts and the most intimate of things just as it indicates His closeness and nearness to all in a manner which befits His majesty.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Doubt in faith  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من أصابه وسوسة في الإيمان</span>

### Fear of shirk

#### 1. `hisn-203`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/203.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/203.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ أَنْ أُشْرِكَ بِكَ وَأَنَا أَعْلَمُ، وَأَسْتَغْفِرُكَ لِمَا لاَ أَعْلَمُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (shirk: to associate others with Allah in those things which are specific to Him.  This can occur in (1) belief, e.g. to believe that other than Allah has the power to benefit or harm, (2) speech, e.g. to swear by other than Allah and (3) action, e.g. to bow or prostrate to other than Allah.)(Allahumma innee a'oothu bika an oshrika bika wa-ana a'lam, wa-astaghfiruka lima la a'lam.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (shirk: to associate others with Allah in those things which are specific to Him.  This can occur in (1) belief, e.g. to believe that other than Allah has the power to benefit or harm, (2) speech, e.g. to swear by other than Allah and (3) action, e.g. to bow or prostrate to other than Allah.)(O Allah, I take refuge in You lest I should commit shirk with You knowingly and I seek Your forgiveness for what I do unknowingly.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Fear of shirk  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء الخوف من الشرك</span>

### Against bad omens

#### 1. `hisn-205`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/205.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/205.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ لاَ طَيْرَ إِلاَّ طَيْرُكَ، وَلاَ خَيْرَ إِلاَّ خَيْرُكَ، وَلاَ إِلَهَ غَيْرُكَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (This supplication is used whenever one initially thinks a casual event or occurrence to foretell good or evil, using it as a basis to determine which action he should undertake, but he then denounces such a link, relies on Allah and then says this supplication as an expiation for this act, since it falls under the category of shirk.)(Allahumma la tayra illa tayruk, wala khayra illa khayruk, wala ilaha ghayruk.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (This supplication is used whenever one initially thinks a casual event or occurrence to foretell good or evil, using it as a basis to determine which action he should undertake, but he then denounces such a link, relies on Allah and then says this supplication as an expiation for this act, since it falls under the category of shirk.)(O Allah, there is no omen but there is reliance on You, there is no good except Your good and none has the right to be worshipped except You.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Against bad omens  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء كراهية الطيرة</span>  

---

### When you feel pain

#### 1. `hisn-243`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/243.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/243.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">ضَعْ يَدَكَ عَلَى الَّذِي تَألَّمَ مِنْ جَسَدِكَ وَقُلْ: بِسْمِ اللَّهِ، ثَلاَثاً، وَقُلْ سَبْعَ مَرَّاتٍ: أَعُوذُ بِاللَّهِ وَقُدْرَتِهِ مِنْ شَرِّ مَا أَجِدُ وَأُحَاذِرُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Place your hand at the site of the pain and say: (Bismil-lah (three times)) the supplicate seven times: (a'oothu billahi waqudratih min sharri ma ajidu wa-ohathir.))  

**Translation (en)**
> (Place your hand at the site of the pain and say: (In the name of Allah(three times the supplicate seven times: (I take refuge in Allah and within His omnipotence from the evil that I feel and am wary of.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When you feel pain  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول من أحس وجعا في جسده</span>

### Fearing you may give the evil eye

#### 1. `hisn-244`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/244.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/244.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِذَا رَأَى أَحَدُكُم مِنْ أَخِيهِ، أَوْ مِنْ نَفْسِهِ، أَوْ مِنْ مَالِهِ مَا يُعْجِبُهُ [فَلْيَدْعُ لَهُ بِالْبَرَكَةِ] فَإِنَّ الْعَيْنَ حَقٌّ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> If you see anything of your brother that pleases you, or of his person or of his property [then ask Allah to bless him in it] for the envious eye is real.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Fearing you may give the evil eye  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من خشي أن يصيب شيئا بعينه</span>

### Seeing someone afflicted

#### 1. `hisn-194`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/194.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/194.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي عَافَانِي مِمَّا ابْتَلاَكَ بِهِ، وَفَضَّلَنِي عَلَى كَثِيرٍ مِمَّنْ خَلَقَ تَفْضِيلاً.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Alhamdu lillahil-lathee 'afanee mimmab-talaka bih, wafaddalanee 'ala katheerin mimman khalaqa tafdeela.)  

**Translation (en)**

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Seeing someone afflicted  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من رأى مبتلى</span>

### After speaking ill of someone

#### 1. `hisn-230`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/230.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/230.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: اللَّهُمَّ فَأَيُّمَا مُؤْمِنٍ سَبَبْتُهُ فَاجْعَلْ ذَلِكَ لَهُ قُرْبَةً إِلَيْكَ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma fa-ayyuma mu/minin sababtuhu faj'al thalika lahu qurbatan ilayka yawmal-qiyamah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, to any believer whom I have insulted, let that be cause to draw him near to You on the Day of Resurrection.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — After speaking ill of someone  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء لمن سببته</span>

### Protection from the Dajjal

#### 1. `hisn-199`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/199.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/199.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">مَنْ حَفِظَ عَشْرَ آيَاتٍ مِنْ أَوَّلِ سُورَةِ الْكَهْفِ عُصِمَ مِنَ الدَّجَّالِ ، وَالْاسْتِعَاذَةُ بِاللَّهِ مِنْ فِتْنَتِهِ عَقِبَ التَّشَهُّدِ الْأَخِيرِ مِنْ كُلِّ صَلاَةٍ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Dajjal: among the great signs of the last hour and the greatest trials to befall mankind, which every Prophet has warned about.  Most of mankind will folow him. He will appear from Asbahan, Iran at the time when Muslims will conquer Constantinople.  He will be given special powers and will make the truth seem false and vice versa. He will claim to be righteous and then he will claim prophet-hood and finally, divinity. From his features is that he will be blind in his right eye which is a definite proof that contradicts his claim to be Allah as it is a sign of imperfection.  The word Kafir will be written between his eyes which every believer, literate or illiterate will recognise.)(Whoever memorises the first ten verses of soorat Al-Kahf will be protected from Dajjal.)(One should also seek refuge with Allah from the tribulations of the Dajjal after the last tashahhud in prayer.(Refer to supplications #55 & #56  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Protection from the Dajjal  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يعصم الله به من الدجال</span>  

---

### Foiling the devil's plots

#### 1. `hisn-247`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/247.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/247.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَعُوذُ بكَلِمَاتِ اللَّهِ التَّامَّاتِ الَّتِي لاَ يُجَاوِزُهُنَّ بَرٌّ وَلاَ فَاجِرٌ: مِنْ شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ، وَبَرَأَ وَذَرَأَ، وَمِنْ شَرِّ مَا يَنْزِلُ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ، وَمِنْ شَرِّ مَا يَعْرُجُ فيهَا، وَمِنْ شَرِّ مَا ذَرَأَ فِي الْأَرْضِ، وَمِنْ شَرِّ مَا يَخْرُجُ مِنْهَا، وَمِنْ شَرِّ فِتَنِ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ، وَمِنْ شَرِّ كُلِّ طَارِقٍ إِلاَّ طَارِقاً يَطْرُقُ بِخَيْرٍ يَا رَحْمَنُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (a'oothu bikalimatil-lahit-tammat, allatee la yujawizuhunna barrun wala fajir min sharri ma khalaq, wabaraa watharaa, wamin sharri ma yanzilu minas-sama/, wamin sharri ma ya'ruju feeha, wamin sharri ma tharaa fil-ard, wamin sharri ma yakhruju minha, wamin sharri fitnanil-layli wannahar, wamin sharri kulli tariqin illa tariqan yatruqu bikhayrin ya Rahman.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (I take refuge within Allah’s perfect words which no righteous or unrighteous person can transgress, from all the evil that He has created, made and originated.  (I take refuge) from the evil that descends from the sky and the evil that rises up to it.  (I take refuge) from the evil that is spread on Earth and the evil that springs from her, and I take refuge from the evil of the tribulations of night and day, and the evil of one who visits at night except the one who brings good, O Merciful One.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Foiling the devil's plots  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — ما يقول لرد كيد مردة الشياطين</span>  

---

### Istikharah — asking for guidance

#### 1. `hisn-74`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/74.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/74.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ جَابرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ عَنْهُمَا: كَانَ رسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُعَلِّمُنَا الْاسْتِخَارَةَ فِي الْأُمُورِ كُلِّهَا كَمَا يُعَلِّمُنَا السُّورَةَ مِنَ الْقُرْآنِ، يَقُولُ: إِذَا هَمَّ أَحَدُكُمْ بِالْأَمْرِ فَلْيَرْكَعْ رَكْعَتَيْنِ مِنْ غَيْرِ الْفَرِيضَةِ، ثُمَّ لْيَقُلْ: اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْتَخِيرُكَ بِعِلْمِكَ، وَأَسْتَقْدِرُكَ بِقُدْرَتِكَ، وَأَسْأَلُكَ مِنْ فَضْلِكَ العَظِيمِ؛ فَإِنَّكَ تَقْدِرُ وَلاَ أَقْدِرُ، وَتَعْلَمُ وَلاَ أَعْلَمُ، وَأَنْتَ عَلاَّمُ الغُيُوبِ، اللَّهُمَّ إِنْ كُنْتَ تَعْلَمُ أَنَّ هَذَا الأمْرَ - وَيُسَمِّي حَاجَتَهُ - خَيْرٌ لِي فِي دِينِي وَمَعَاشِي وَعَاقِبَةِ أَمْرِي – أَوْ قَالَ: عَاجِلِهِ وَآجِلِهِ - فَاقْدُرْهُ لِي وَيَسِّرْهُ لِي ثمَّ بَارِكْ لِي فِيهِ، وَإِنْ كُنْتَ تَعْلَمُ أَنَّ هَذَا الْأَمْرَ شَرٌّ لِي فِي دِينِي وَمَعَاشِي وَعَاقِبَةِ أَمْرِي – أَوْ قَالَ: عَاجِلِهِ وَآجِلِهِ – فَاصْرِفْهُ عَنِّي وَاصْرِفْنِي عَنْهُ وَاقْدُرْ لِيَ الْخَيْرَ حَيْثُ كَانَ، ثُمَّ أَرْضِنِي بِهِ.وَمَا نَدِمَ مَنِ اسْتَخَارَ الْخَالِقَ، وَشَاوَرَ الْمَخْلُوقِينَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَتَثَبَّتَ فِي أَمْرِهِ، فَقَدْ قَالَ اللَّه تعالى: ﴿وَشاوِرْهُمْ فِي الْأَمْرِ فَإِذَا عَزَمْتَ فَتَوَكَّلْ عَلَى اللَّهِ﴾ .</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (On the authority of Jabir Ibn 'abdullah , he said: ’The Prophet  would instruct us to pray for guidance in all of our concerns, just as he would teach us a chapter from the Quran.  He  would say ’ If any of you intends to undertake a matter then let him pray two supererogatory units (two rak'ah nafilah) of prayer and after which he should supplicate:Allahumma innee astakheeruka bi'ilmik, wa-astaqdiruka biqudratik, wa-as-aluka min fadlikal-'atheem, fa-innaka taqdiru wala aqdir, wata'lamu wala a'lam ,wa-anta 'allamul ghuyoob, allahumma in kunta ta'lamu anna hathal-amr (say your need) khayrun lee fee deenee wama'ashee wa'aqibati amree faqdurhu lee, wayassirhu lee, thumma barik lee feeh, wa-in kunta ta'lamu anna hathal-amr sharrun lee fee deenee wama'ashee wa'aqibati amree fasrifhu 'annee wasrifnee 'anh, waqdur liyal-khayra haythu kan, thumma ardinee bih.)(One who seeks guidance from his Creator and consults his fellow believers and then remains firm in his resolve does not regret, for Allah has said:washawirhum fee al-amri fa-itha 'azamta fatawakkal 'ala Allah)(chapter 3 verse 159)  

**Translation (en)**
> (On the authority of Jabir Ibn AAabdullah , he said: ’The Prophet  would instruct us to pray for guidance in all of our concerns, just as he would teach us a chapter from the Quran.  He  would say ’ If any of you intends to undertake a matter then let him pray two supererogatory units (two rakAAah nafilah) of prayer and after which he should supplicate:‘O Allah, I seek Your counsel by Your knowledge and by Your power I seek strength and I ask You from Your immense favour, for verily You are able while I am not and verily You know while I do not and You are the Knower of the unseen.  O Allah, if You know this affair -and here he mentions his need- to be good for me in relation to my religion, my life, and end, then decree and facilitate it for me, and bless me with it, and if You know this affair to be ill for me towards my religion, my life, and end, then remove it from me and remove me from it, and decree for me what is good wherever it be and make me satisfied with such.)(One who seeks guidance from his Creator and consults his fellow believers and then remains firm in his resolve does not regret, for Allah has said:…and consult them in the affair. Then when you have taken a decision, put your trust in Allah…)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Istikharah — asking for guidance  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء صلاة الاستخارة</span>

### Facing an adversary or a ruler

#### 1. `hisn-126`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَنْتَ عَضُدِي، وَأَنْتَ نَصِيرِي، بِكَ أَحُولُ وَبِكَ أَصُولُ، وَبِكَ أُقاتِلُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**

### Against an unjust ruler

#### 1. `hisn-129`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/129.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/129.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ ربَّ السَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعِ، وَرَبَّ الْعَرْشِ الْعَظِيمِ، كُنْ لِي جَاراً مِنْ فُلاَنِ بْنِ فُلاَنٍ، وَأَحْزَابِهِ مِنْ خَلاَئِقِكَ، أَنْ يَفْرُطَ عَلَيَّ أَحَدٌ مِنْهُمْ أَوْ يَطْغَى، عَزَّ جَارُكَ، وَجَلَّ ثَنَاؤُكَ، وَلاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أَنْتَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allaahumma rabbas-samaawaatis-sab'i, wa Rabbal-'Arshil-'Adheem, Kun lee jaaran min (here you mention the person's name) wa 'ahzabihi min khalaa'iqika, 'an yafruta 'alayya 'ahadun minhum 'aw yatghaa,'Azza jaaruka, wa jalla thanaa'uka, wa laa 'ilaaha 'illaa 'Anta.  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, Lord of the seven heavens, Lord of the Magnificent Throne,  be for me a support against [such and such a person] and his helpers from among your creatures, lest any of them abuse me or do me wrong. Mighty is your patronage and glorious are your praises. There is none worthy of worship but you.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Against an unjust ruler  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من خاف ظلم السلطان</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-130`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/130.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/130.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللَّهُ أَعَزُّ مِنْ خَلْقِهِ جَمِيعاً، اللَّهُ أَعَزُّ مِمَّا أَخَافُ وَأَحْذَرُ، أَعُوذُ بِاللَّهِ الَّذِي لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ هُوَ، الْمُمْسِكِ السَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعِ أَنْ يَقَعْنَ عَلَى الْأَرْضِ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِهِ، مِنْ شَرِّ عَبْدِكَ فُلاَنٍ، وَجُنُودِهِ وَأَتْبَاعِهِ وَأَشْيَاعِهِ، مِنْ الْجِنِّ وَالإِنْسِ، اللَّهُمَّ كُنْ لِي جَاراً مِنْ شَرِّهِمْ، جَلَّ ثَنَاؤُكَ وَعَزَّ جَارُكَ، وَتَبَارَكَ اسْمُكَ، وَلاَ إِلَهَ غَيْرُكَ (ثلاثَ مرَّاتٍ).</span>  

**Transliteration**
> Allaahu Akbar, Allaahu 'a'azzu min khalqihi jamee'an, Allaahu 'a'azzu mimmaa 'akhaafu wa 'ahdharu, 'a'oothu billaahil-lathee laa ilaaha illaa huwa, almumsikis-samaawaatis-sab'i 'an yaqa'na 'alaal-'ardhi 'illaa bi'ithnihi, min sharri 'abdika (name of the person) wa junoodihi wa 'atbaa'ihi wa ashyaa'ihi minal-jinni wal'insi, Allaahumma kun lee jaaran min sharrihim, Jalla thanaa'uka wa 'azza aaruka, wa tabaarakasmuka, wa laa ilaaha ghayruka.  

**Translation (en)**
> Allah is the Most Great, Mightier than all His creation. He is Mightier than what fear and dread. I seek refuge in Allah, Who there is none worthy of worship but Him. He is the One Who holds the seven heavens from falling upon the earth except by His command. [I seek refuge in You Allah] from the evil of Your slave [name of the person], and his helpers, his followers and his supporters from among the jinn and mankind. O Allah, be my support against their evil. Glorious Your praises and mighty is Your patronage. Blessed is Your Name, there is no true God but You. (Recite three times in Arabic)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Against an unjust ruler  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من خاف ظلم السلطان</span>

### Visiting the sick

#### 1. `hisn-147`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/147.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/147.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ بأْسَ طَهُورٌ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ba'sa tahoorun in shaal-lah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Never mind, may it (the sickness) be a purification, if Allah wills.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Visiting the sick  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للمريض في عيادته</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-148`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/148.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/148.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">أَسْأَلُ اللَّهَ الْعَظيمَ رَبَّ الْعَرْشِ الْعَظِيمِ أَنْ يَشْفيَكَ (سبع مرات).</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Asalul-lahal-'atheem rabbal-'arshil-'atheem an yashfeek (7times).)  

**Translation (en)**
> (I ask Allah The Supreme, Lord of the magnificent throne to cure you’.(7times) (he (the sick person) will be cured.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Visiting the sick  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للمريض في عيادته</span>

### The reward for visiting the sick

#### 1. `hisn-149`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/149.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/149.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: إِذَا عَادَ الرَّجُلُ أَخَاهُ الْمُسْلِمَ مَشَى فِي خِرَافَةِ الْجَنَّةِ حَتَّى يَجْلِسَ، فَإِذَا جَلَسَ غَمَرَتْهُ الرَّحْمَةُ، فَإِنْ كَانَ غُدْوَةً صَلَّى عَلَيْهِ سَبْعُونَ أَلْفَ مَلَكٍ حَتَّى يُمْسِيَ، وَإِنْ كَانَ مَسَاءً صَلَّى عَلَيْهِ سَبْعُونَ أَلْفَ مَلَكٍ حَتَّى يُصْبِحَ.</span>  

**Translation (en)**
> (Ali Ibn Abee Talib  related that he heard the Messenger of Allah  say: ‘If a man calls on his sick Muslim brother, it is as if he walks reaping the fruits of Paradise until he sits, and when he sits he is showered in mercy, and if this was in the morning, seventy thousand angles send prayers upon him until the evening, and if this was in the evening, seventy thousand angles send prayers upon him until the morning.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The reward for visiting the sick  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — فضل عيادة المريض</span>

### For someone near the end

#### 1. `hisn-150`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/150.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/150.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي، وَارْحَمْنِي، وَأَلْحِقْنِي بِالرَّفِيقِ الْأَعْلَى.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummagh-fir lee, warhamnee wa-alhiqnee birrafeeqil-a'la.)  

**Translation (en)**
> ‘O Allaah, forgive me, have mercy upon me and unite me with the highest companions  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For someone near the end  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء المريض الذي يئس من حياته</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-151`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/151.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/151.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">جَعَلَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم عِنْدَ مَوْتِهِ يُدْخِلُ يَدَيْهِ فِي الْمَاءِ فَيَمْسَحُ بِهِمَا وَجْهَهُ، وَيَقُولُ: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ إِنَّ لِلْمَوْتِ سَكَرَاتٍ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> 'Aa.ishah (radhi-yAllaahu 'anhaa) related that The Prophet (sal-Allaahu 'alayhe wa sallam) [during his illness in which he passed away] would dip his hands in water and then he would wipe his face and say:[La ilaha illal-lah, inna lilmawti lasakarat.] ‘None has the right to be worshipped except Allaah, death does indeed contain agony.’  

**Translation (en)**
> 'Aa.ishah (radhi-yAllaahu 'anhaa) related that The Prophet (sal-Allaahu 'alayhe wa sallam) [during his illness in which he passed away] would dip his hands in water and then he would wipe his face and say: ‘None has the right to be worshipped except Allaah, death does indeed contain agony.’  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For someone near the end  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء المريض الذي يئس من حياته</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-152`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/152.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/152.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ لَهُ المُلْكُ وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ وَلاَ حَوْلَ وَلاَ قُوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللَّهِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (La ilaha illal-lah, wallahu akbar, la ilaha illal-lahu wahdah, la shareeka lah, la ilaha illal-lahu lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, la ilaha illal-lah, wala hawla wala quwwata illa billah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah and Allah is the greatest.None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone.None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, alone, without partner.None has the right to be worshipped except Allah, to Him belongs all sovereignty and praise.  None has the right to be worshipped except Allah and there is no might and no power except with Allah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — For someone near the end  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء المريض الذي يئس من حياته</span>

### Prompting the dying

#### 1. `hisn-153`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">مَنْ كَانَ آخِرُ كَلاَمِهِ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ دَخَلَ الْجَنَّةَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> i.e. those around the sick should instruct and encourage him to say the shahadah. (He whose last words are: (La ilaha illal-lah.) will enter Paradise.)  

**Translation (en)**
> i.e. those around the sick should instruct and encourage him to say the shahadah. (He whose last words are: (None has the right to be worshipped except Allah.) will enter Paradise.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Prompting the dying  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — تلقين المحتضر</span>

### When tragedy strikes

#### 1. `hisn-154`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/154.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/154.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِنَّا لِلَّهِ وَإِنَّا إِلَيْهِ رَاجِعُونَ، اللَّهُمَّ أْجُرْنِي فِي مُصِيبَتِي، وَأَخْلِفْ لِي خَيْرَاً مِنْهَا.</span>

**Translation (en)**

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — When tragedy strikes  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء من أصيب بمصيبة</span>
