### Closing the eyes of the dead

#### 1. `hisn-155`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/155.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/155.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِفُلاَنٍ (بِاسْمِهِ) وَارْفَعْ دَرَجَتَهُ فِي الْمَهْدِيِّينَ، وَاخْلُفْهُ فِي عَقِبِهِ فِي الْغَابِرِينَ، وَاغْفِرْ لَنَا وَلَهُ  يَا رَبَّ الْعَالَمِينَ، وَافْسَحْ لَهُ فِي قَبْرِهِ، وَنَوِّرْ لَهُ فِيهِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummagh-fir li-name the dead- warfa' darajatahu fil-mahdiyyeen, wakhlufhu fee 'aqibihi fil-ghabireen, waghfir lana walahu ya rabbal-'alameen wafsah lahu fee qabrih, wanawwir lahu feeh.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, forgive -here the name of the deceased is mentioned- and raise his rank among the rightly guided, and be a successor to whom he has left behind, and forgive us and him O Lord of the worlds.  Make spacious his grave and illuminate it for him.)(A successor: one who succeeds another due to the latter’s absence or death. This is the correct meaning of the word khaleefah; thus, it is incorrect to believe that Adam is the khaleefah (vicegerent, as is commonly translated) of Allah on earth because Allah is never absent, and will never die.  This supplication proves the correct understanding of this term and shows that Allah succeeds us and guards whom we leave behind when we die or are absent (also refer to supplication #198).)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Closing the eyes of the dead  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند إغماض الميت</span>  

---

### The funeral prayer

#### 1. `hisn-156`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/156.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/156.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لَهُ وَارْحَمْهُ، وَعَافِهِ، وَاعْفُ عَنْهُ، وَأَكْرِمْ نُزُلَهُ، وَوَسِّعْ مُدْخَلَهُ، وَاغْسِلْهُ بِالْمَاءِ وَالثَّلْجِ وَالْبَرَدِ، وَنَقِّهِ مِنَ الْخَطَايَا كَمَا نَقَّيْتَ الثَّوْبَ الأَبْيَضَ مِنَ الدَّنَسِ، وَأَبْدِلْهُ دَاراً خَيْراً مِنْ دَارِهِ، وَأَهْلاً خَيْراً مِنْ أَهْلِهِ، وَزَوْجَاً خَيْراً مِنْ زَوْجِهِ، وَأَدْخِلْهُ الْجَنَّةَ، وَأَعِذْهُ مِنْ عَذَابِ القَبْرِ [وَعَذَابِ النَّارِ].</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummagh-fir lahu warhamh, wa'afihi, wa'fu 'anh, wa-akrim nuzulah, wawassi' mudkhalah, waghsilhu bilma-i waththalji walbarad, wanaqqihi minal-khataya kama naqqaytath-thawbal-abyada minad-danas, wa-abdilhu daran khayran min darih, wa-ahlan khayran min ahlih wazawjan khayran min zawjih, wa-adkhilhul-jannah, wa-a'ithhu min 'athabil-qabr, wa'athabin-nar)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, forgive and have mercy upon him, excuse him and pardon him, and make honourable his reception. Expand his entry, and cleanse him with water, snow, and ice, and purify him of sin as a white robe is purified of filth.  Exchange his home for a better home, and his family for a better family, and his spouse for a better spouse. Admit him into the Garden, protect him from the punishment of the grave and the torment of the Fire.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للميت في الصلاة عليه</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-157`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/157.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/157.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِحَيِّنَا وَمَيِّتِنَا، وَشَاهِدِنَا وَغَائِبِنَا، وَصَغِيرِنَا وَكَبيرِنَا، وَذَكَرِنَا وَأُنْثَانَا. اللَّهُمَّ مَنْ أَحْيَيْتَهُ مِنَّا فَأَحْيِهِ عَلَى الْإِسْلاَمِ، وَمَنْ تَوَفَّيْتَهُ مِنَّا فَتَوَفَّهُ عَلَى الإِيمَانِ، اللَّهُمَّ لاَ تَحْرِمْنَا أَجْرَهُ، وَلاَ تُضِلَّنَا بَعْدَهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummagh-fir lihayyina wamayyitina washahidina, wagha-ibina, wasagheerina wakabeerina, wathakarina wa-onthana. Allahumma man ahyaytahu minna fa-ahyihi 'alal-islam, waman tawaffaytahu minna fatawaffahu 'alal-eeman, allahumma la tahrimna ajrah, wala tudillana ba'dah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, forgive our living and our dead, those present and those absent, our young and our old, our males and our females.  O Allah, whom amongst us You keep alive, then let such a life be upon Islam, and whom amongst us You take unto Yourself, then let such a death be upon faith.  O Allah, do not deprive us of his reward and do not let us stray after him.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للميت في الصلاة عليه</span>  

---

#### 3. `hisn-158`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/158.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/158.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّ فُلاَنَ بْنَ فُلاَنٍ فِي ذِمَّتِكَ، وَحَبْلِ جِوَارِكَ، فَقِهِ مِنْ فِتْنَةِ الْقَبْرِ، وَعَذَابِ النَّارِ، وَأَنْتَ أَهْلُ الْوَفَاءِ وَالْحَقِّ، فَاغْفِرْ لَهُ وَارْحَمْهُ إِنَّكَ أَنْتَ الغَفُورُ الرَّحيمُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma inna -name the dead- fee thimmatik, wahabli jiwarik, faqihi min fitnatil-qabr wa'athabin-nar, wa-anta ahlul-wafa/, walhaq, faghfir lahu warhamh, innaka antal-ghafoorur-raheem.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, so-and-so is under Your care and protection so protect him from the trial of the grave and torment of the Fire.  Indeed You are faithful and truthful. Forgive and have mercy upon him, surely You are The Oft-Forgiving, The Most-Merciful.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للميت في الصلاة عليه</span>  

---

#### 4. `hisn-159`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/159.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/159.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ عَبْدُكَ وَابْنُ أَمَتِكَ احْتَاجَ إِلَى رَحْمَتِكَ، وَأَنْتَ غَنِيٌّ عَنْ عَذَابِهِ، إِنْ كَانَ مُحْسِناً فَزِدْ فِي حَسَنَاتِهِ، وَإِنْ كَانَ مُسِيئاً فَتَجَاوَزْ عَنْهُ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahumma 'abduka wabnu amatik, ihtaja ila rahmatik, wa-anta ghaniyyun 'an 'athabih, in kana muhsinan fazid fee hasanatih, wa-in kana museean fatajawaz 'anh.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, Your servant and the son of Your maidservant is in need of Your mercy and You are without need of his punishment. If he was righteous then increase his reward and if he was wicked then look over his sins.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للميت في الصلاة عليه</span>  

---

### The funeral prayer for a child

#### 1. `hisn-160`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/160.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/160.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ أَعِذْهُ مِنْ عَذَابِ القَبْرِ وإن قال: اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْهُ فَرَطاً وَذُخْراً لِوَالِدَيْهِ، وَشَفِيعاً مُجَاباً، اللَّهُمَّ ثَقِّلْ بِهِ مَوَازِينَهُمَا، وَأَعْظِمْ بِهِ أُجورَهُمَا، وَأَلْحِقْهُ بِصَالِحِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، وَاجْعَلْهُ فِي كَفَالَةِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، وَقِهِ بِرَحْمَتِكَ عَذَابَ الْجَحِيمِ، وَأَبْدِلْهُ دَاراً خَيْراً مِنْ دَارِهِ، وَأَهْلاً خَيْراً مِنْ أَهْلِهِ، اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِأَسْلاَفِنَا، وَأَفْرَاطِنَا، وَمَنْ سَبَقَنَا بِالْإِيمَانِ إن قال ذلك فَحَسَنٌ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allaahumma 'a'ith-hu min 'athaabil-qabri.) [or say:](Allahummaj-'alhu faratan, wathukhran liwalidayh, washafee'an mujaban. Allahumma thaqqil bihi mawazeenahuma wa-a'thim bihi ojoorahuma, wa-alhiqhu bisalihil-mu'mineen, waj'alhu fee kafalati Ibraheem, waqihi birahmatika 'athabal-jaheem, wa 'abdilhu daaran khayran min daarihi, wa 'ahlan khayran min 'ahlihi, Allaahum-maghfir li'aslaafinaa, wa afraatinaa wa man sabaqanaa bil'eemaan)  

**Translation (en)**
> O Allah, protect him from the torment of the grave . [it is also good to say] O Allah, make him a precursor, a forerunner and a treasure for his parents and an answered intercessor. O Allah, make him weigh heavily in their scales (of good)  and magnify their reward. Make him join the righteous of the believers. Place him in the care of Ibrahim. Save him by Your mercy from the torment of Hell. Give him a home better than his home and a family better than his family. O Allah, forgive those who have gone (i.e. passed away) before us, our children lost (by death), and those who have preceded us in Faith.  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer for a child  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للفرط في الصلاة عليه</span>  

---

#### 2. `hisn-161`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/161.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/161.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْهُ لَنَا فَرَطاً، وَسَلَفاً، وَأَجْراً.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Allahummaj-'alhu lana farata, wasalafan wa-ajra.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (O Allah, make him for us a precursor,a forerunner and a cause of reward)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — The funeral prayer for a child  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء للفرط في الصلاة عليه</span>

### Condolence

#### 1. `hisn-162`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/162.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/162.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">إِنَّ للَّهِ مَا أَخَذَ، وَلَهُ مَا أَعْطَى، وَكُلُّ شَيْءٍ عِنْدَهُ بِأَجَلٍ مُسَمَّى... فَلْتَصْبِرْ وَلْتَحْتَسِبْ. وَإِنْ قَالَ: أَعْظَمَ اللَّهُ أَجْرَكَ، وَأَحْسَنَ عَزَاءَكَ، وَغَفَرَ لِمَيِّتِكَ فَحَسَنٌ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Inna lillahi ma akhath, walahu ma a'ta, wakullu shayin 'indahu bi-ajalin musamma…faltasbir waltahtasib.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Verily to Allah, belongs what He took and to Him belongs what He gave, and everything with Him has an appointed time…and then he  ordered for her to be patient and hope for Allah’s reward.)(The words (faltasbir waltahtasib) are commands in the feminine 3rd person form, so they will need to be changed in respect to whom is being addressed.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Condolence  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء التعزية</span>  

---

### Placing the dead in the grave

#### 1. `hisn-163`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/163.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/163.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">بِسْمِ اللَّهِ وَعَلَى سُنَّةِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Bismil-lahi wa'ala sunnati rasoolil-lah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (In the name of Allah and upon the sunnah of the Messenger of Allah.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Placing the dead in the grave  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — الدعاء عند إدخال الميت القبر</span>

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)

### Visiting the graves

#### 1. `hisn-165`

**Attribution**: Prophetic (from the Prophet ﷺ)  
**Repeat**: 1×  
**Audio**: [https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/165.mp3](https://www.hisnmuslim.com/audio/ar/165.mp3)

**Arabic**
> <span dir="rtl">السَّلاَمُ عَلَيْكُمْ أَهْلَ الدِّيَارِ، مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَالْمُسْلِمِينَ، وَإِنَّا إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ بِكُمْ لاَحِقُونَ، [وَيَرْحَمُ اللَّهُ الْمُسْتَقدِمِينَ مِنَّا وَالْمُسْتأْخِرِينَ] أَسْاَلُ اللَّهَ لَنَا وَلَكُمُ الْعَافِيَةَ.</span>  

**Transliteration**
> (Assalamu 'alaykum ahlad-diyari minal-mu/mineena walmuslimeen, wa-inna in shaal-lahu bikum lahiqoon, nas-alul-laha lana walakumul-'afiyah.)  

**Translation (en)**
> (Peace be upon you all, O inhabitants of the graves, amongst the believers and the Muslims. Verily we will, Allah willing, be united with you, we ask Allah for well-being for us and you.)  

**Reference**
> Hisn al-Muslim — Visiting the graves  

**Reference (ar)**
> <span dir="rtl">حصن المسلم — دعاء زيارة القبور</span>  

---
